const DASHBOARD_ROLES = ["SYSTEM_ADMIN", "OWNER", "WARDEN"];
const ALL_HOSTELS_VALUE = "__ALL_HOSTELS__";
const REQUIRED_COUNTS = [
  "totalStudents",
  "activeStudents",
  "totalRooms",
  "vacantRooms",
  "totalBeds",
  "occupiedBeds",
  "vacantBeds",
  "reservedBeds",
  "maintenanceBeds",
  "todayEntry",
  "todayExit",
  "pendingOutpasses",
  "openComplaints"

function App() {
  const { user, token, logout, updateUser, selectedHostelId: activeHostelId, setSelectedHostelId: setActiveHostelId } = useAuth();
  const [summary, setSummary] = useState(null);
  const [hostelInfo, setHostelInfo] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("loading");
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState("");
  const [reloadKey, setReloadKey] = useState(0);
  const [rangeDays, setRangeDays] = useState(7);
  const [lastUpdated, setLastUpdated] = useState(null);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [refreshError, setRefreshError] = useState("");
  const hasSummaryRef = useRef(false);
  const requestInProgressRef = useRef(false);
  const [section, setSection] = useState("dashboard");
  const [notificationUnreadCount, setNotificationUnreadCount] = useState(0);
  const [allocationStudentId, setAllocationStudentId] = useState("");
  const [registerAfterSignOut, setRegisterAfterSignOut] = useState(false);
  const [editingOwnerProfile, setEditingOwnerProfile] = useState(false);
  const [accessibleHostels, setAccessibleHostels] = useState([]);
  const [hostelListError, setHostelListError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      !token ||
      !["OWNER", "WARDEN"].includes(user?.role) ||
      activeHostelId === ALL_HOSTELS_VALUE
    ) {
      setNotificationUnreadCount(0);
      return undefined;
    }

    let active = true;
    const controller = new AbortController();

    const refreshUnreadCount = async () => {
      try {
        const response = await apiRequest("/api/notifications", {
          token,
          signal: controller.signal
        });

        if (active) {
          setNotificationUnreadCount(
            Math.max(0, Number(response?.data?.unreadCount) || 0)
          );
        }
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (active && requestError.name !== "AbortError") {
          // Keep the last known count if a temporary request fails.
        }
      }
    };

    void refreshUnreadCount();

    const intervalId = window.setInterval(() => {
      void refreshUnreadCount();
    }, 15000);

    return () => {
      active = false;
      controller.abort();
      window.clearInterval(intervalId);
    };
  }, [token, user?.role, activeHostelId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    hasSummaryRef.current = false;
    requestInProgressRef.current = false;
    setSummary(null);
    setLastUpdated(null);
    setRefreshing(false);
    setRefreshError("");
    setErrorMessage("");
    setAccessibleHostels([]);
    setHostelListError("");
  }, [token, user?.role]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!token || !DASHBOARD_ROLES.includes(user?.role)) {
      setAccessibleHostels([]);
      return undefined;
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    let active = true;

    apiRequest("/api/auth/hostels", { token, signal: controller.signal })
      .then((payload) => {
        if (!active) return;

        const items = Array.isArray(payload?.data?.items)
          ? payload.data.items.filter((h) =>
              h && typeof h._id === "string" && typeof h.name === "string"
            )
          : [];

        setAccessibleHostels(items);
        setHostelListError("");

        const isAdmin = user.role === "SYSTEM_ADMIN";
        const selectedAllowed = isAdmin
          ? activeHostelId === ALL_HOSTELS_VALUE ||
            items.some((h) => h._id === activeHostelId)
          : items.some((h) => h._id === activeHostelId);

        if (!selectedAllowed) {
          setActiveHostelId(
            isAdmin
              ? ALL_HOSTELS_VALUE
              : items.find((h) => h._id === user.hostelId)?._id || items[0]?._id || ""
          );
        }
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        if (active && error.name !== "AbortError") {
          setHostelListError(error.message || "Unable to load accessible hostels.");
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [token, user?.role, user?.hostelId, activeHostelId, setActiveHostelId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!token || !DASHBOARD_ROLES.includes(user?.role)) {
      setStatus("restricted");
      return undefined;
    }

    if (!hasSummaryRef.current) {
      setStatus("loading");
      setErrorMessage("");
    } else {
      setRefreshing(true);
      setRefreshError("");
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    let active = true;
    requestInProgressRef.current = true;

    const loadSummary = async () => {
      try {
        const payload = await apiRequest(`/api/dashboard/summary?rangeDays=${rangeDays}`, {
          token,
          signal: controller.signal
        });

        if (!payload?.data?.counts ||
            REQUIRED_COUNTS.some((key) => !Number.isFinite(payload.data.counts[key])) ||
            !Array.isArray(payload.data.recentStudents) ||
            !Array.isArray(payload.data.recentEntryExit) ||
            !Array.isArray(payload.data.recentComplaints)) {
          throw new Error("The dashboard response was incomplete. Retry or contact support.");
        }

        if (
          payload.data.analytics?.rangeDays !== rangeDays ||
          !Array.isArray(payload.data.analytics?.dailyGateActivity) ||
          payload.data.analytics.dailyGateActivity.length !== rangeDays ||
          !payload.data.analytics.dailyGateActivity.every((point) =>
            point && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(point.date) &&
            Number.isFinite(point.entries) && Number.isFinite(point.exits) &&
            point.entries >= 0 && point.exits >= 0
          )
        ) {
          throw new Error("Dashboard trend response was incomplete. Retry or contact support.");
        }

        if (!active) return;

        hasSummaryRef.current = true;
        setSummary(payload.data);
        setLastUpdated(new Date());
        setRefreshError("");
        setErrorMessage("");
        setStatus("ready");
      } catch (error) {
        if (error.name !== "AbortError" && active) {
          if (error.status === 401) {
            logout();
          } else if (hasSummaryRef.current) {
            setRefreshError(error.message || "Dashboard refresh failed. Showing the last successful data.");
          } else {
            setErrorMessage(error.message || "Unable to load dashboard records.");
            setStatus("error");
          }
        }
      } finally {
        if (active) {
          requestInProgressRef.current = false;
          setRefreshing(false);
        }
      }
    };

    loadSummary();

    return () => {
      active = false;
      controller.abort();
      requestInProgressRef.current = false;
    };
  }, [token, user?.role, logout, reloadKey, rangeDays, activeHostelId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!token || !DASHBOARD_ROLES.includes(user?.role) || section !== "dashboard") {
      return undefined;
    }

    const intervalId = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (!requestInProgressRef.current) {
        setReloadKey((key) => key + 1);
      }
    }, 60_000);

    return () => window.clearInterval(intervalId);
  }, [token, user?.role, section, activeHostelId]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const supportedRole = ["SYSTEM_ADMIN", "OWNER", "WARDEN"].includes(user?.role);
    if (
      !token ||
      !supportedRole ||
      !activeHostelId ||
      activeHostelId === ALL_HOSTELS_VALUE
    ) {
      setHostelInfo(null);
      return undefined;
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", { token, signal: controller.signal })
      .then((response) => setHostelInfo(response.data))
      .catch((error) => {
        if (error.name !== "AbortError") setHostelInfo(null);
      });

    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token, user?.role, activeHostelId, reloadKey]);

  if (!user) {
    return <Login initialMode={registerAfterSignOut ? "register" : "login"} onRegistrationStart={() => setRegisterAfterSignOut(false)} />;
  }

  if (user.role === "STUDENT") {
    return (<Suspense fallback={<main className="auth-page qr-page"><section className="auth-card">Loading student QR...</section></main>}><StudentQrPage token={token} logout={logout} /></Suspense>);
  }

  if (user.role === "GUARD") {
    return (<Suspense fallback={<main className="auth-page qr-page"><section className="auth-card">Loading gate scanner...</section></main>}><GateScannerPage token={token} logout={logout} /></Suspense>);
  }

  if (user.role === "PARENT") {
    return (
      <Suspense fallback={<main className="auth-page"><section className="auth-card">Loading family portal...</section></main>}>
        <ParentPortalPage token={token} logout={logout} />
      </Suspense>
    );
  }
  if (!DASHBOARD_ROLES.includes(user.role)) {
    return (
      <main className="auth-page">
        <section className="auth-card access-card">
          <div className="auth-brand-mark"><Home size={28} aria-hidden="true" /></div>
          <h1>Dashboard Not Available</h1>
          <p className="auth-description">
            Your {user.role.replaceAll("_", " ").toLowerCase()} account is active, but this dashboard is limited to hostel administrators and wardens.
          </p>
          <button className="auth-submit" type="button" onClick={logout}>Sign Out</button>
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (status !== "ready" || !summary) {
    return (
      <main className="auth-page">
        <section className="auth-card access-card">
          <h1>{status === "error" ? "Dashboard Unavailable" : "Loading Dashboard..."}</h1>
          <p className="auth-description" role="status" aria-live="polite">
            {status === "error" ? errorMessage : "Loading current hostel records from the server..."}
          </p>
          {status === "error" ? (
            <button
              className="auth-submit"
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setReloadKey((key) => key + 1)}
            >

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Building2, Bed, MapPin, Mail, Phone, Search, X, RefreshCw, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, MessageCircle, Navigation, IndianRupee } from "lucide-react";
import { API_BASE_URL, apiRequest } from "../services/api.js";
import "../styles/public-hostels.css";

const imageUrl = (value) => {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !value.trim()) return "";
  const path = value.trim();

  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(path)) return path;
  if (path.startsWith("/")) return `${API_BASE_URL.replace(/\/+$/, "")}${path}`;

  return "";
};

const locationText = (hostel) =>
  [hostel.city, hostel.state, hostel.pincode].filter(Boolean).join(", ") ||
  "Location not provided";

const addressText = (hostel) =>
  [hostel.address, hostel.city, hostel.state, hostel.pincode]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ");

const PRICE_OPTIONS = [
  { key: "singleSharing", label: "Single sharing" },
  { key: "doubleSharing", label: "Double sharing" },
  { key: "tripleSharing", label: "Triple sharing" },
  { key: "fourSharing", label: "Four sharing" }
];

const listedPrices = (pricing) =>
  PRICE_OPTIONS
    .map(({ key, label }) => ({ key, label, amount: pricing?.[key] }))
    .filter(({ amount }) =>
      amount !== "" &&
      amount !== null &&
      amount !== undefined &&
      Number.isFinite(Number(amount)) &&
      Number(amount) > 0
    )
    .map((item) => ({ ...item, amount: Number(item.amount) }));

const formatMoney = (amount) =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat("en-IN", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "INR",
    maximumFractionDigits: 0
  }).format(Number(amount));

const googleMapsUrl = (hostel) => {
  const location = locationText(hostel);
  const query = addressText(hostel) ||
    [hostel.name, location !== "Location not provided" ? location : ""]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(", ");
  return `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`;
};

const whatsappUrl = (contact) => {
  let digits = String(contact || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (digits.length === 10) digits = `91${digits}`;
  return digits.length >= 10 && digits.length <= 15
    ? `https://wa.me/${digits}`
    : "";
};
function HostelPhoto({ src, alt, className = "" }) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const source = imageUrl(src);

  useEffect(() => setFailed(false), [source]);

  if (!source || failed) {
    return (
      <div className={`${className} public-hostel-photo-fallback`} aria-label={alt}>
        <Building2 size={36} aria-hidden="true" />
        <span>Hostel photo</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <img
      className={className}
      src={source}
      alt={alt}
      loading="lazy"
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    />
  );
}

function HostelDetails({ hostel, onClose }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };

    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [onClose]);

  const setup = hostel.roomSetup || {};
  const gallery = Array.isArray(hostel.galleryImages)
    ? hostel.galleryImages.filter((item) => imageUrl(item))
    : [];

  return (
    <div
      className="public-hostel-modal-backdrop"
      onMouseDown={(event) => {
        if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
    >
      <section
        className="public-hostel-modal"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="public-hostel-details-title"
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          className="public-hostel-modal-close"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Close hostel details"
        >
          <X size={20} />
        </button>

        <HostelPhoto
          src={hostel.photoUrl}
          alt={`${hostel.name} hostel`}
          className="public-hostel-modal-cover"
        />

        <div className="public-hostel-modal-content">
          <span className="public-hostel-kicker">HOSTEL PROFILE</span>
          <h2 id="public-hostel-details-title">{hostel.name}</h2>
          <p className="public-hostel-location">
            <MapPin size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
            {locationText(hostel)}
          </p>

          <div className="public-hostel-detail-grid">
            <div>
              <span>Full address</span>
              <strong>{addressText(hostel) || "Not provided"}</strong>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>Contact number</span>
              {hostel.contact ? (
                <a href={`tel:${hostel.contact}`}>{hostel.contact}</a>
              ) : (
                <strong>Not provided</strong>
              )}
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>Email address</span>
              {hostel.email ? (
                <a href={`mailto:${hostel.email}`}>{hostel.email}</a>
              ) : (
                <strong>Not provided</strong>
              )}
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>Listed since</span>
              <strong>
                {hostel.createdAt
                  ? new Date(hostel.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()
                  : "Not available"}
              </strong>
            </div>
          </div>

          <section className="public-hostel-pricing" aria-label="Hostel rent and charges">
            <h3><IndianRupee size={19} aria-hidden="true" /> Rent and charges</h3>
            {listedPrices(hostel.pricing).length > 0 ? (
              <div className="public-hostel-pricing-list">
                {listedPrices(hostel.pricing).map(({ key, label, amount }) => (
                  <div className="public-hostel-pricing-item" key={key}>
                    <span>{label}</span>
                    <strong>{formatMoney(amount)} <small>/ month</small></strong>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="public-hostel-price-note">
                Monthly rent not listed. Contact the hostel for current prices.
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="public-hostel-extra-charges">
              <div>
                <span>Security deposit</span>
                <strong>{hostel.pricing?.securityDeposit != null ? formatMoney(hostel.pricing.securityDeposit) : "Confirm with hostel"}</strong>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Mess charges / month</span>
                <strong>{hostel.pricing?.messCharges != null ? formatMoney(hostel.pricing.messCharges) : "Not specified"}</strong>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span>Mess included in rent</span>
                <strong>
                  {hostel.pricing?.messIncluded === true
                    ? "Yes"
                    : hostel.pricing?.messIncluded === false
                      ? "No"
                      : "Confirm with hostel"}
                </strong>
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>
          <div className="public-hostel-action-row">
            {hostel.contact ? (
              <a className="public-hostel-action" href={`tel:${String(hostel.contact).replace(/[^\d+]/g, "")}`}>
                <Phone size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Call hostel
              </a>
            ) : null}
            {whatsappUrl(hostel.contact) ? (
              <a className="public-hostel-action public-hostel-action-whatsapp" href={whatsappUrl(hostel.contact)} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
                <MessageCircle size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> WhatsApp
              </a>
            ) : null}
            {(addressText(hostel) || locationText(hostel) !== "Location not provided") ? (
              <a className="public-hostel-action public-hostel-action-maps" href={googleMapsUrl(hostel)} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
                <Navigation size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Google Maps
              </a>
            ) : null}
          </div>          <h3>Room setup</h3>
          <div className="public-hostel-stats">
            <div><strong>{Number(setup.totalRooms) || 0}</strong><span>Total rooms</span></div>
            <div><strong>{Number(setup.totalBeds) || 0}</strong><span>Total beds</span></div>
            <div><strong>{Number(setup.bedsPerRoom) || 0}</strong><span>Beds per room</span></div>
          </div>

          {Array.isArray(setup.roomTypes) && setup.roomTypes.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="public-hostel-room-types">
              <span>Room types</span>
              <div>
                {setup.roomTypes.map((type) => (
                  <span className="public-hostel-chip" key={type}>{type}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {gallery.length > 0 ? (
            <>
              <h3>Hostel gallery</h3>
              <div className="public-hostel-gallery">
                {gallery.map((photo, index) => (
                  <HostelPhoto
                    key={`${photo}-${index}`}
                    src={photo}
                    alt={`${hostel.name} gallery ${index + 1}`}
                    className="public-hostel-gallery-photo"
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          ) : null}

          <p className="public-hostel-disclaimer">
            Details are supplied by the hostel owner. Room setup figures may describe
            the registered profile and are not a live vacancy guarantee.
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function PublicHostelDirectory() {
  const [hostels, setHostels] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [searchText, setSearchText] = useState("");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [pagination, setPagination] = useState({ page: 1, limit: 9, total: 0, pages: 0 });
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null);
  const [reloadKey, setReloadKey] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    const params = new URLSearchParams({
      page: String(page),
      limit: "9"
    });
    if (query) params.set("q", query);

    apiRequest(`/api/hostels/public?${params.toString()}`, {
      signal: controller.signal
    })
      .then((response) => {
        setHostels(response.data?.items || []);
        setPagination(response.data?.pagination || { page, limit: 9, total: 0, pages: 0 });
      })
      .catch((requestError) => {
        if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message);
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [page, query, reloadKey]);

  const search = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setPage(1);
    setQuery(searchText.trim());
  };

  return (
    <section
      className="public-hostels-section"
      id="registered-hostels"
      aria-labelledby="public-hostels-heading"

        <div className="public-hostels-heading">

            <p>Explore hostel profiles registered on Smart Hostel Management System.</p>
          </div>

          <button
            className="public-hostel-refresh"
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setReloadKey((value) => value + 1)}
            disabled={loading}

            <RefreshCw size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
            Refresh
          </button>
        </div>

            <input
              type="search"
              placeholder="Search by hostel name, city, state or address"
              value={searchText}
              onChange={(event) => setSearchText(event.target.value)}
              aria-label="Search registered hostels"

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Building2, Bed, MapPin, Mail, Phone, Search, X, RefreshCw, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { API_BASE_URL, apiRequest } from "../services/api.js";
import "../styles/public-hostels.css";

const imageUrl = (value) => {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !value.trim()) return "";
  const path = value.trim();

  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(path)) return path;
  if (path.startsWith("/")) return `${API_BASE_URL.replace(/\/+$/, "")}${path}`;

  return "";
};

const locationText = (hostel) =>
  [hostel.city, hostel.state, hostel.pincode].filter(Boolean).join(", ") ||
  "Location not provided";

const addressText = (hostel) =>
  [hostel.address, hostel.city, hostel.state, hostel.pincode]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ");

function HostelPhoto({ src, alt, className = "" }) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const source = imageUrl(src);

  useEffect(() => setFailed(false), [source]);

  if (!source || failed) {
    return (
      <div className={`${className} public-hostel-photo-fallback`} aria-label={alt}>
        <Building2 size={36} aria-hidden="true" />
        <span>Hostel photo</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <img
      className={className}
      src={source}
      alt={alt}
      loading="lazy"
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    />
  );
}

function HostelDetails({ hostel, onClose }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };

    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [onClose]);

  const setup = hostel.roomSetup || {};
  const gallery = Array.isArray(hostel.galleryImages)
    ? hostel.galleryImages.filter((item) => imageUrl(item))
    : [];

  return (
    <div
      className="public-hostel-modal-backdrop"
      onMouseDown={(event) => {
        if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
    >
      <section
        className="public-hostel-modal"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="public-hostel-details-title"
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          className="public-hostel-modal-close"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Close hostel details"
        >
          <X size={20} />
        </button>

        <HostelPhoto
          src={hostel.photoUrl}
          alt={`${hostel.name} hostel`}
          className="public-hostel-modal-cover"
        />

        <div className="public-hostel-modal-content">
          <span className="public-hostel-kicker">HOSTEL PROFILE</span>
          <h2 id="public-hostel-details-title">{hostel.name}</h2>
          <p className="public-hostel-location">
            <MapPin size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
            {locationText(hostel)}
          </p>

          <div className="public-hostel-detail-grid">
            <div>
              <span>Full address</span>
              <strong>{addressText(hostel) || "Not provided"}</strong>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>Contact number</span>
              {hostel.contact ? (
                <a href={`tel:${hostel.contact}`}>{hostel.contact}</a>
              ) : (
                <strong>Not provided</strong>
              )}
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>Email address</span>
              {hostel.email ? (
                <a href={`mailto:${hostel.email}`}>{hostel.email}</a>
              ) : (
                <strong>Not provided</strong>
              )}
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>Listed since</span>
              <strong>
                {hostel.createdAt
                  ? new Date(hostel.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()
                  : "Not available"}
              </strong>
            </div>
          </div>

          <h3>Room setup</h3>
          <div className="public-hostel-stats">
            <div><strong>{Number(setup.totalRooms) || 0}</strong><span>Total rooms</span></div>
            <div><strong>{Number(setup.totalBeds) || 0}</strong><span>Total beds</span></div>
            <div><strong>{Number(setup.bedsPerRoom) || 0}</strong><span>Beds per room</span></div>
          </div>

          {Array.isArray(setup.roomTypes) && setup.roomTypes.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="public-hostel-room-types">
              <span>Room types</span>
              <div>
                {setup.roomTypes.map((type) => (
                  <span className="public-hostel-chip" key={type}>{type}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {gallery.length > 0 ? (
            <>
              <h3>Hostel gallery</h3>
              <div className="public-hostel-gallery">
                {gallery.map((photo, index) => (
                  <HostelPhoto
                    key={`${photo}-${index}`}
                    src={photo}
                    alt={`${hostel.name} gallery ${index + 1}`}
                    className="public-hostel-gallery-photo"
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          ) : null}

          <p className="public-hostel-disclaimer">
            Details are supplied by the hostel owner. Room setup figures may describe
            the registered profile and are not a live vacancy guarantee.
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

            <p>Explore hostel profiles registered on Smart Hostel Management System.</p>
          </div>

          <button
            className="public-hostel-refresh"
            type="button"

            disabled={loading}
          >
            <RefreshCw size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
            Refresh
          </button>

FILE: .\client\src\context\AuthContext.jsx.bak_hostelcontext_20261016_121911
======================================================================
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { apiRequest } from "../services/api.js";

const AuthContext = createContext(null);
const SESSION_STORAGE_KEY = "shms.authSession";

const readStoredSession = () => {
  try {
    const stored = window.sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!stored) return null;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(stored);
    if (
      typeof parsed?.token === "string" &&
      parsed.token &&
      parsed.user &&
      typeof parsed.user.id === "string" &&
      typeof parsed.user.role === "string"
    ) {
      return parsed;
    }
  } catch {
    window.sessionStorage.removeItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
  }
  return null;
};

const storeSession = (session) => {
  try {
    if (session) {
      window.sessionStorage.setItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(session));
    } else {
      window.sessionStorage.removeItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
    }
  } catch {
    window.sessionStorage.removeItem(SESSION_STORAGE_KEY);
  }
};

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(() => readStoredSession());

  const login = useCallback(async (credentials) => {
    const response = await apiRequest("/api/auth/login", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(credentials)
    });
    setSession(response.data);
    storeSession(response.data);
    return response.data.user;
  }, []);

  const registerOwner = useCallback(async (profile) => {
    const response = await apiRequest("/api/auth/register", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(profile)
    });
    setSession(response.data);
    storeSession(response.data);
    return response.data.user;
  }, []);

  const logout = useCallback(() => {
    setSession(null);
    storeSession(null);
  }, []);
  const updateUser = useCallback((user) => setSession((current) => {
    if (!current) return current;
    const next = { ...current, user: { ...current.user, ...user } };
    storeSession(next);
    return next;
  }), []);
  const value = useMemo(() => ({
    token: session?.token || null,
    user: session?.user || null,
    login,
    registerOwner,
    logout,
    updateUser
  }), [session, login, registerOwner, logout, updateUser]);

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth() {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useAuth must be used inside AuthProvider.");
  }
  return context;
}

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { ArrowRightLeft, BedDouble, Check, DoorOpen, RefreshCw, Trash2, UserRound } from "lucide-react";
import { apiRequest } from "../services/api.js";

const today = () => new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
function AllocationManager({ token, initialStudentId = "", onComplete }) {
  const [blocks, setBlocks] = useState([]); const [floors, setFloors] = useState([]); const [rooms, setRooms] = useState([]);
  const [students, setStudents] = useState([]); const [beds, setBeds] = useState([]); const [allocations, setAllocations] = useState([]);
  const [hostel, setHostel] = useState(null); const [blockId, setBlockId] = useState(""); const [floorId, setFloorId] = useState(""); const [roomId, setRoomId] = useState("");
  const [studentId, setStudentId] = useState(initialStudentId); const [studentQuery, setStudentQuery] = useState(""); const [bedId, setBedId] = useState(""); const [allocationDate, setAllocationDate] = useState(today());
  const [mode, setMode] = useState("assign"); const [current, setCurrent] = useState(null); const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true); const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false); const [error, setError] = useState(""); const [notice, setNotice] = useState(""); const [reload, setReload] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => { if (initialStudentId) setStudentId(initialStudentId); }, [initialStudentId]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController(); setLoading(true); setError("");
    Promise.all([
      apiRequest("/api/allocations/options", { token, signal: controller.signal }),
      apiRequest("/api/allocations?limit=100", { token, signal: controller.signal }),
      apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", { token, signal: controller.signal }).catch(() => null)
    ]).then(([options, currentAllocations, hostelResult]) => {
      setBlocks(options.data.blocks); setFloors(options.data.floors); setRooms(options.data.rooms); setAllocations(currentAllocations.data.items); setHostel(hostelResult?.data || null);
      if (!blockId && options.data.blocks.length) setBlockId(options.data.blocks[0]._id);
    }).catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); }).finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token, reload]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    Promise.all([
      apiRequest(`/api/facilities/rooms?limit=100`, { token, signal: controller.signal }),
      apiRequest(`/api/students?status=ACTIVE&limit=20${studentQuery.trim() ? `&q=${encodeURIComponent(studentQuery.trim())}` : ""}`, { token, signal: controller.signal })
    ]).then(([roomResult, studentResult]) => {
      setRooms(roomResult.data.items);
      setStudents(studentResult.data.items.filter((student) => !student.bedId || String(student._id) === studentId));
    }).catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token, studentQuery, studentId, reload]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!roomId) { setBeds([]); setBedId(""); return undefined; }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    apiRequest(`/api/allocations/available-beds?roomId=${encodeURIComponent(roomId)}`, { token, signal: controller.signal })
      .then((response) => { setBeds(response.data); if (!response.data.some((bed) => String(bed._id) === bedId)) setBedId(""); })
      .catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") { setBeds([]); setError(requestError.message); } });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token, roomId, reload]);
  const availableFloors = useMemo(() => floors.filter((floor) => String(floor.blockId) === String(blockId)), [floors, blockId]);
  const availableRooms = useMemo(() => rooms.filter((room) => String(room.floorId) === String(floorId) && String(room.blockId) === String(blockId) && !["MAINTENANCE", "RESERVED"].includes(room.status)), [rooms, floorId, blockId]);
  const selectedRoom = rooms.find((room) => String(room._id) === String(roomId));
  const occupiedCount = allocations.filter((allocation) => String(allocation.roomId) === String(roomId)).length;
  const roomAvailable = selectedRoom ? Math.max(0, selectedRoom.capacity - occupiedCount) : 0;
  const reloadData = () => setReload((value) => value + 1);
  const chooseTransfer = (allocation) => { setCurrent(allocation); setMode("transfer"); setStudentId(String(allocation.studentId?._id || allocation.studentId)); const oldBed = allocation.bedId; const oldRoom = oldBed?.roomId?._id || oldBed?.roomId; const room = rooms.find((item) => String(item._id) === String(oldRoom)); if (room) { setBlockId(String(room.blockId)); setFloorId(String(room.floorId)); setRoomId(""); } setBedId(""); setNotice(""); setError(""); };
  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault(); if (saving) return;
    setSaving(true); setError(""); setNotice("");
    try {
      const payload = { studentId, roomId, bedId, allocationDate };
      const response = mode === "transfer"
        ? await apiRequest(`/api/allocations/${current._id}/transfer`, { token, method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ roomId, bedId }) })
        : await apiRequest("/api/allocations", { token, method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(payload) });
      setNotice(`Student successfully ${mode === "transfer" ? "transferred to" : "assigned to"} Room ${response.data.room.roomNumber} / Bed ${response.data.bed.bedNumber}.`);
      setMode("assign"); setCurrent(null); setBedId(""); setStudentId(""); setAllocationDate(today()); reloadData(); onComplete?.();
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };
  const deleteAllocation = async (allocation) => {
    const student = allocation.studentId?.name || allocation.studentId?.studentId || "this resident";
    if (!window.confirm(`Delete ${student} from current allocations and release their bed? Allocation history will be retained.`)) return;
    setError(""); setNotice("");
    try { await apiRequest(`/api/allocations/${allocation._id}`, { token, method: "DELETE" }); setNotice(`${student} was removed from current allocations. Allocation history has been retained.`); reloadData(); onComplete?.(); }
    catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
  };
  return <section className="facility-manager allocation-page" aria-labelledby="allocation-heading">
    <div className="facility-heading-row"><div><span className="dashboard-kicker">HOSTEL STRUCTURE & ALLOCATION</span><h2 id="allocation-heading">Assign Resident</h2><p>Hostel <strong>{hostel?.name || "Current verified hostel"}</strong> <span className="hierarchy-crumb">â†’ Block â†’ Floor â†’ Room â†’ Bed â†’ Student</span></p></div><button className="facility-secondary" type="button" onClick={reloadData}><RefreshCw size={16} /> Refresh</button></div>
    <div className="allocation-grid">
      <form className="facility-panel allocation-form" onSubmit={submit}>
        <div className="facility-form-heading"><div><h3>{mode === "transfer" ? "Transfer Student" : "Assign Resident"}</h3><p>{mode === "transfer" ? `Current: ${current?.bedId?.roomId?.roomNumber || "Room"} / ${current?.bedId?.bedNumber || "Bed"}` : "Select an active, unallocated student and an available bed."}</p></div></div>
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Student Search</span><input type="search" value={studentQuery} onChange={(event) => setStudentQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search by name or student ID" /></label>
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Student</span><select required value={studentId} disabled={mode === "transfer"} onChange={(event) => setStudentId(event.target.value)}><option value="">Select active student</option>{students.map((student) => <option key={student._id} value={student._id}>{student.name} Â· {student.studentId}</option>)}</select></label>
        <div className="allocation-student-summary"><UserRound size={17} /><span>{students.find((student) => String(student._id) === String(studentId)) ? `${students.find((student) => String(student._id) === String(studentId)).name} Â· ${students.find((student) => String(student._id) === String(studentId)).studentId}` : "No student selected"}</span></div>
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Block</span><select required value={blockId} onChange={(event) => { setBlockId(event.target.value); setFloorId(""); setRoomId(""); }}><option value="">Select block</option>{blocks.map((block) => <option key={block._id} value={block._id}>{block.name}</option>)}</select></label>
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Floor</span><select required value={floorId} onChange={(event) => { setFloorId(event.target.value); setRoomId(""); }}><option value="">Select floor</option>{availableFloors.map((floor) => <option key={floor._id} value={floor._id}>{floor.name || `Floor ${floor.number}`}</option>)}</select></label>
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Room</span><select required value={roomId} onChange={(event) => setRoomId(event.target.value)}><option value="">Select room</option>{availableRooms.map((room) => <option key={room._id} value={room._id}>Room {room.roomNumber} Â· Capacity {room.capacity}</option>)}</select></label>
        {selectedRoom ? <div className="room-capacity-card"><div className="capacity-icon"><DoorOpen size={18} /></div><div><strong>Room {selectedRoom.roomNumber}</strong><span>Capacity {selectedRoom.capacity} Â· Occupied {occupiedCount} Â· Available {roomAvailable}</span></div><span className="capacity-status">{selectedRoom.status}</span><small>Available beds: {beds.length}</small></div> : null}
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Bed</span><select required value={bedId} disabled={!roomId || !beds.length} onChange={(event) => setBedId(event.target.value)}><option value="">{!roomId ? "Select a room first" : beds.length ? "Select available bed" : "No available beds"}</option>{beds.map((bed) => <option key={bed._id} value={bed._id}>Bed {bed.bedNumber} Â· AVAILABLE</option>)}</select></label>
        {mode === "assign" ? <label className="facility-field"><span>Allocation Date</span><input type="date" max={today()} value={allocationDate} onChange={(event) => setAllocationDate(event.target.value)} /></label> : null}
        {error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}
        <button className="facility-submit" type="submit" disabled={saving || loading || !studentId || !roomId || !bedId}>{saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : mode === "transfer" ? <><ArrowRightLeft size={16} /> Transfer Student</> : <><Check size={16} /> Assign Bed</>}</button>
        {mode === "transfer" ? <button className="facility-secondary" type="button" onClick={() => { setMode("assign"); setCurrent(null); setBedId(""); }}>Cancel transfer</button> : null}
      </form>
      <section className="facility-panel current-allocation-panel"><div className="facility-form-heading"><div><h3>Current Allocations</h3><p>Active bed assignments for this hostel.</p></div><span className="allocation-total">{allocations.length}</span></div>{loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading allocationsâ€¦</p> : null}{!loading && allocations.length === 0 ? <p className="facility-empty">No records yet</p> : null}<div className="active-allocation-list">{allocations.map((allocation) => <article className="active-allocation" key={allocation._id}><div className="allocation-person"><span className="allocation-avatar"><UserRound size={16} /></span><div><strong>{allocation.studentId?.name || "Resident"}</strong><small>{allocation.studentId?.studentId || "Student ID unavailable"} Â· {allocation.studentId?.mobile || ""}</small></div></div><div className="allocation-location"><BedDouble size={15} /><span>Room {allocation.bedId?.roomId?.roomNumber || "â€”"} / Bed {allocation.bedId?.bedNumber || "â€”"}</span><small>Since {new Date(allocation.allocationDate).toLocaleDateString()}</small></div><div className="allocation-actions"><button type="button" onClick={() => chooseTransfer(allocation)}><ArrowRightLeft size={14} /> Transfer</button><button className="archive" type="button" onClick={() => deleteAllocation(allocation)}><Trash2 size={14} /> Delete</button></div></article>)}</div></section>
    </div>
  </section>;
}
export default AllocationManager;

"Transfer Student" : "Assign Resident"}</h3><p>{mode === "transfer" ? `Current: ${current?.bedId?.roomId?.roomNumber || "Room"} / ${current?.bedId?.bedNumber || "Bed"}` : "Select an active, unallocated student and an available bed."}</p></div></div>
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Student Search</span><input type="search" value={studentQuery} onChange={(event) => setStudentQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search by name or student ID" /></label>
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Student</span><select required value={studentId} disabled={mode === "transfer"} onChange={(event) => setStudentId(event.target.value)}><option value="">Select active student</option>{students.map((student) => <option key={student._id} value={student._id}>{student.name} · {student.studentId}</option>)}</select></label>
        <div className="allocation-student-summary"><UserRound size={17} /><span>{students.find((student) => String(student._id) === String(studentId)) ? `${students.find((student) => String(student._id) === String(studentId)).name} · ${students.find((student) => String(student._id) === String(studentId)).studentId}` : "No student selected"}</span></div>
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Block</span><select required value={blockId} onChange={(event) => { setBlockId(event.target.value); setFloorId(""); setRoomId(""); }}><option value="">Select block</option>{blocks.map((block) => <option key={block._id} value={block._id}>{block.name}</option>)}</select></label>
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Floor</span><select required value={floorId} onChange={(event) => { setFloorId(event.target.value); setRoomId(""); }}><option value="">Select floor</option>{availableFloors.map((floor) => <option key={floor._id} value={floor._id}>{floor.name || `Floor ${floor.number}`}</option>)}</select></label>
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Room</span><select required value={roomId} onChange={(event) => setRoomId(event.target.value)}><option value="">Select room</option>{availableRooms.map((room) => <option key={room._id} value={room._id}>Room {room.roomNumber} · Capacity {room.capacity}</option>)}</select></label>
        {selectedRoom ? <div className="room-capacity-card"><div className="capacity-icon"><DoorOpen size={18} /></div><div><strong>Room {selectedRoom.roomNumber}</strong><span>Capacity {selectedRoom.capacity} · Occupied {occupiedCount} · Available {roomAvailable}</span></div><span className="capacity-status">{selectedRoom.status}</span><small>Available beds: {beds.length}</small></div> : null}
        <label className="facility-field"><span>Bed</span><select required value={bedId} disabled={!roomId || !beds.length} onChange={(event) => setBedId(event.target.value)}><option value="">{!roomId ? "Select a room first" : beds.length ? "Select available bed" : "No available beds"}</option>{beds.map((bed) => <option key={bed._id} value={bed._id}>Bed {bed.bedNumber} · AVAILABLE</option>)}</select></label>
        {mode === "assign" ? <label className="facility-field"><span>Allocation Date</span><input type="date" max={today()} value={allocationDate} onChange={(event) => setAllocationDate(event.target.value)} /></label> : null}
        {error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}
        <button className="facility-submit" type="submit" disabled={saving || loading || !studentId || !roomId || !bedId}>{saving ? "Saving…" : mode === "transfer" ? <><ArrowRightLeft size={16} /> Transfer Student</> : <><Check size={16} /> Assign Bed</>}</button>
        {mode === "transfer" ? <button className="facility-secondary" type="button" onClick={() => { setMode("assign"); setCurrent(null); setBedId(""); }}>Cancel transfer</button> : null}
      </form>
      <section className="facility-panel current-allocation-panel"><div className="facility-form-heading"><div><h3>Current Allocations</h3><p>Active bed assignments for this hostel.</p></div><span className="allocation-total">{allocations.length}</span></div>{loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading allocations…</p> : null}{!loading && allocations.length === 0 ? <p className="facility-empty">No records yet</p> : null}<div className="active-allocation-list">{allocations.map((allocation) => <article className="active-allocation" key={allocation._id}><div className="allocation-person"><span className="allocation-avatar"><UserRound size={16} /></span><div><strong>{allocation.studentId?.name || "Resident"}</strong><small>{allocation.studentId?.studentId || "Student ID unavailable"} · {allocation.studentId?.mobile || ""}</small></div></div><div className="allocation-location"><BedDouble size={15} /><span>Room {allocation.bedId?.roomId?.roomNumber || "—"} / Bed {allocation.bedId?.bedNumber || "—"}</span><small>Since {new Date(allocation.allocationDate).toLocaleDateString()}</small></div><div className="allocation-actions"><button type="button" onClick={() => chooseTransfer(allocation)}><ArrowRightLeft size={14} /> Transfer</button><button className="archive" type="button" onClick={() => release(allocation)}><LogOut size={14} /> Release</button></div></article>)}</div></section>
    </div>
  </section>;
}
export default AllocationManager;

FILE: .\client\src\pages\FacilityManager.jsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Archive, Building2, DoorOpen, Layers3, Plus, RefreshCw, Save, X } from "lucide-react";
import { apiRequest } from "../services/api.js";
import PhotoField from "../components/PhotoField.jsx";

const RESOURCES = [
  { id: "blocks", label: "Blocks", icon: Building2 },
  { id: "floors", label: "Floors", icon: Layers3 },
  { id: "rooms", label: "Rooms", icon: DoorOpen },
  { id: "beds", label: "Beds", icon: Archive }
];
const EMPTY = { name: "", blockId: "", number: "", floorId: "", roomNumber: "", roomType: "Standard", capacity: "1", roomId: "", bedNumber: "", status: "AVAILABLE" };
const LABELS = { name: "Block name", blockId: "Block", number: "Floor number", floorId: "Floor", roomNumber: "Room number", roomType: "Room type", capacity: "Bed capacity", roomId: "Room", bedNumber: "Bed number", status: "Status" };

const PUBLIC_PRICING_FIELDS = [
  { key: "singleSharing", label: "Single sharing rent / month" },
  { key: "doubleSharing", label: "Double sharing rent / month" },
  { key: "tripleSharing", label: "Triple sharing rent / month" },
  { key: "fourSharing", label: "Four sharing rent / month" },
  { key: "securityDeposit", label: "Security deposit" },
  { key: "messCharges", label: "Mess charges / month" }
];

function FacilityManager({ token, role, hasTenant, onHostelChanged }) {
  const [resource, setResource] = useState("blocks");
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [parents, setParents] = useState({ blocks: [], floors: [], rooms: [] });
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY);
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState("");
  const [showProfile, setShowProfile] = useState(false);
  const [selectedRecord, setSelectedRecord] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    Promise.all(["blocks", "floors", "rooms"].map(async (key) => {
      const result = await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${key}?limit=100`, { token, signal: controller.signal });
      return [key, result.data.items];
    })).then((records) => setParents(Object.fromEntries(records)))
      .catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token, refreshKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}?limit=100${query.trim() ? `&q=${encodeURIComponent(query.trim())}` : ""}`, { token, signal: controller.signal })
      .then((result) => setItems(result.data.items))
      .catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); })
      .finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [resource, query, token, refreshKey]);

  const fields = useMemo(() => {
    if (resource === "blocks") return ["name"];
    if (resource === "floors") return ["blockId", "number", "name"];
    if (resource === "rooms") return ["blockId", "floorId", "roomNumber", "roomType", "capacity", "status"];
    return ["roomId", "bedNumber", "status"];
  }, [resource]);

  const clearForm = () => { setForm(EMPTY); setEditingId(""); };
  const startEdit = (record) => {
    setEditingId(record._id);
    setForm({ ...EMPTY, ...record, blockId: record.blockId || "", floorId: record.floorId || "", roomId: record.roomId || "" });
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
  };

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    const payload = {};
    for (const field of fields) {
      if (form[field] === "" || form[field] === undefined) continue;
      payload[field] = ["number", "capacity"].includes(field) ? Number(form[field]) : form[field];
    }
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}${editingId ? `/${editingId}` : ""}`, {
        token,
        method: editingId ? "PATCH" : "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      setNotice(editingId ? "Facility updated." : "Facility created.");
      clearForm();
      setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1);
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.message);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const deleteFacility = async (record) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Delete ${record.name || record.roomNumber || record.bedNumber || "this facility"} from active hostel records? This is a soft delete; historical data is retained and dependent or occupied records are protected.`)) return;
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}/${record._id}`, { token, method: "DELETE" });
      setNotice("Facility deleted from active records. Historical data is retained.");
      setSelectedRecord(null);
      if (editingId === record._id) clearForm();
      setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1);
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.message);
    }
  };

  const view = async (record) => {
    setError("");
    try {
      const result = await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}/${record._id}`, { token });
      setSelectedRecord(result.data);
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
  };

  const relatedOptions = (field) => field === "blockId" ? parents.blocks : field === "floorId" ? parents.floors.filter((item) => !form.blockId || String(item.blockId) === String(form.blockId)) : parents.rooms;
  const labelFor = (record) => resource === "blocks" ? record.name : resource === "floors" ? (record.name || `Floor ${record.number}`) : resource === "rooms" ? record.roomNumber : record.bedNumber;
  if (showProfile) return <HostelProfile token={token} onBack={() => setShowProfile(false)} onSaved={onHostelChanged} />;

  return (
    <section className="facility-manager" aria-labelledby="facility-heading">
      <div className="facility-heading-row">
        <div><span className="dashboard-kicker">HOSTEL CONFIGURATION</span><h2 id="facility-heading">Facility Management</h2><p>Manage real blocks, floors, rooms, and beds in the selected hostel.</p></div>
        <div className="facility-heading-actions">{role === "OWNER" || (role === "SYSTEM_ADMIN" && hasTenant) ? <button type="button" className="facility-secondary" onClick={() => setShowProfile(true)}><Building2 size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Hostel profile</button> : null}<button type="button" className="facility-secondary" onClick={() => setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1)}><RefreshCw size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Refresh</button></div>
      </div>
      <div className="facility-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Facility type">
        {RESOURCES.map(({ id, label, icon: Icon }) => <button key={id} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={resource === id} className={resource === id ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => { setResource(id); clearForm(); setSelectedRecord(null); setNotice(""); setError(""); }}><Icon size={17} aria-hidden="true" />{label}</button>)}
      </div>
      <div className="facility-layout">
        <section className="facility-panel" aria-label={`${resource} records`}>
          <div className="facility-list-toolbar"><label htmlFor="facility-search">Search {resource}<input id="facility-search" type="search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search current hostel records" /></label><span>{items.length} shown</span></div>
          {loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading {resource} from the serverâ€¦</p> : null}
          {!loading && !error && items.length === 0 ? <p className="facility-empty">No records yet</p> : null}
          {!loading && items.length > 0 ? <div className="facility-record-list">{items.map((record) => <article className="facility-record" key={record._id}><div><strong>{labelFor(record)}</strong><span>{resource === "floors" ? `Block ${parents.blocks.find((item) => String(item._id) === String(record.blockId))?.name || "â€”"}` : resource === "rooms" ? `${record.roomType || "Room"} Â· Capacity ${record.capacity}` : resource === "beds" ? `${parents.rooms.find((item) => String(item._id) === String(record.roomId))?.roomNumber || "Room â€”"} Â· ${record.status}` : record.status}</span></div><div className="facility-row-actions"><button type="button" onClick={() => view(record)}>View</button><button type="button" onClick={() => startEdit(record)}>Edit</button><button type="button" className="archive" onClick={() => deleteFacility(record)}>Delete</button></div></article>)}</div> : null}
          {selectedRecord ? <aside className="student-details" aria-label="Facility details"><strong>{labelFor(selectedRecord)}</strong>{Object.entries(selectedRecord).filter(([key, value]) => !["_id", "__v", "hostelId", "isDeleted", "createdAt", "updatedAt"].includes(key) && value !== null && typeof value !== "object").map(([key, value]) => <span key={key}>{key}: {String(value)}</span>)}</aside> : null}
        </section>
        <form className="facility-panel facility-form" onSubmit={submit}>
          <div className="facility-form-heading"><div><h3>{editingId ? `Edit ${resource.slice(0, -1)}` : `Add ${resource.slice(0, -1)}`}</h3><p>Changes are saved to the hostel database.</p></div>{editingId ? <button type="button" className="facility-icon-button" aria-label="Cancel edit" onClick={clearForm}><X size={17} /></button> : null}</div>
          {fields.map((field) => <label className="facility-field" key={field}><span>{LABELS[field]}</span>{["blockId", "floorId", "roomId", "status"].includes(field) ? <select required={field !== "status"} value={form[field]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value, ...(field === "blockId" ? { floorId: "" } : {}) }))}><option value="">{field === "status" ? "Use default status" : `Select ${LABELS[field].toLowerCase()}`}</option>{field === "status" ? ["AVAILABLE", "RESERVED", "MAINTENANCE"].map((status) => <option key={status} value={status}>{status}</option>) : relatedOptions(field).map((item) => <option key={item._id} value={item._id}>{field === "blockId" ? item.name : field === "floorId" ? (item.name || `Floor ${item.number}`) : item.roomNumber}</option>)}</select> : <input required={!["name"].includes(field) || resource === "blocks"} type={field === "capacity" || field === "number" ? "number" : "text"} min={field === "capacity" ? 1 : field === "number" ? -10 : undefined} max={field === "capacity" ? 500 : field === "number" ? 1000 : undefined} value={form[field]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value }))} />}</label>)}
          {error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}
          <button type="submit" className="facility-submit" disabled={saving}><Save size={16} aria-hidden="true" />{saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : editingId ? "Save changes" : <><Plus size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Add facility</>}</button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


function HostelProfile({ token, onBack, onSaved }) {
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: "", address: "", city: "", state: "", pincode: "", contact: "", email: "", photoUrl: "", paymentSettings: { upiId: "", payeeName: "" }, pricing: { singleSharing: "", doubleSharing: "", tripleSharing: "", fourSharing: "", securityDeposit: "", messCharges: "", messIncluded: "unknown" } });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", { token, signal: controller.signal }).then((response) => {
      setProfile(response.data);
      setForm({
        name: response.data.name || "",
        address: response.data.address || "",
        city: response.data.city || "",
        state: response.data.state || "",
        pincode: response.data.pincode || "",
        contact: response.data.contact || "",
        email: response.data.email || "",
        photoUrl: response.data.photoUrl || "",
        pricing: {
          singleSharing: response.data.pricing?.singleSharing ?? "",
          doubleSharing: response.data.pricing?.doubleSharing ?? "",
          tripleSharing: response.data.pricing?.tripleSharing ?? "",
          fourSharing: response.data.pricing?.fourSharing ?? "",
          securityDeposit: response.data.pricing?.securityDeposit ?? "",
          messCharges: response.data.pricing?.messCharges ?? "",
          messIncluded: response.data.pricing?.messIncluded === true
            ? "yes"
            : response.data.pricing?.messIncluded === false
              ? "no"
              : "unknown"
        }
      });
    }).catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); }).finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token]);
  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true); setError(""); setNotice("");
    try {
      const amountFields = [
        "singleSharing", "doubleSharing", "tripleSharing", "fourSharing",
        "securityDeposit", "messCharges"
      ];
      const payload = {
        ...form,
        pricing: {
          ...Object.fromEntries(amountFields.map((key) => [
            key,
            form.pricing[key] === "" || form.pricing[key] == null
              ? null
              : Number(form.pricing[key])
          ])),
          messIncluded: form.pricing.messIncluded === "yes"
            ? true
            : form.pricing.messIncluded === "no"
              ? false
              : null
        }
      };

      const response = await apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", {
        token,
        method: "PATCH",
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      setProfile(response.data); setNotice("Hostel profile updated."); onSaved?.();
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };
  return <section className="facility-manager" aria-labelledby="hostel-profile-heading"><div className="facility-heading-row"><div><span className="dashboard-kicker">HOSTEL SETTINGS</span><h2 id="hostel-profile-heading">Hostel Profile</h2><p>{profile?.name || "Current hostel"} Â· update contact and location information.</p></div><button className="facility-secondary" type="button" onClick={onBack}>Back to facilities</button></div><form className="facility-panel facility-profile-form" onSubmit={submit}>{loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading hostel profileâ€¦</p> : null}{!loading ? <><div className="hostel-identity-details"><strong>Hostel Name: {profile?.name}</strong><span>Hostel ID: {profile?._id}</span><span>Hostel Code: {profile?.hostelCode || "Not available"}</span>{profile?.accessCode ? <span>Hostel Access Code: {profile.accessCode}</span> : null}</div><PhotoField token={token} subject="hostel" label="Hostel Photo" value={form.photoUrl} onChange={(photoUrl) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, photoUrl }))} />{Object.entries({ name: "Hostel name", address: "Address", city: "City", state: "State / Union Territory", pincode: "Pincode", contact: "Contact number", email: "Email address" }).map(([key, label]) => <label className="facility-field" key={key}><span>{label}</span><input required={key === "name"} type={key === "email" ? "email" : "text"} value={form[key]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: event.target.value }))} /></label>)}{PUBLIC_PRICING_FIELDS.map(({ key, label }) => (
  <label className="facility-field" key={key}>
    <span>{label} (INR)</span>
    <input
      type="number"
      min={key === "securityDeposit" || key === "messCharges" ? 0 : 1}
      max="10000000"
      step="1"
      value={form.pricing[key]}
      onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({
        ...current,
        pricing: { ...current.pricing, [key]: event.target.value }
      }))}
    />
  </label>
))}
<label className="facility-field">
  <span>Mess included in rent?</span>
  <select
    value={form.pricing.messIncluded}
    onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      pricing: { ...current.pricing, messIncluded: event.target.value }
    }))}
  >
    <option value="unknown">Not specified</option>
    <option value="yes">Yes</option>
+
  </select>
</label></> : null}{error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}<button className="facility-submit" type="submit" disabled={loading || saving}><Save size={16} aria-hidden="true" />{saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : "Save hostel profile"}</button></form></section>;
}
export default FacilityManager;

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Archive, Building2, DoorOpen, Layers3, Plus, RefreshCw, Save, X } from "lucide-react";
import { apiRequest } from "../services/api.js";
import PhotoField from "../components/PhotoField.jsx";

  { id: "blocks", label: "Blocks", icon: Building2 },
  { id: "floors", label: "Floors", icon: Layers3 },
  { id: "rooms", label: "Rooms", icon: DoorOpen },
  { id: "beds", label: "Beds", icon: Archive }
];

  }, [token, refreshKey]);

    setNotice("");
  };

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    const payload = {};
    for (const field of fields) {
      if (form[field] === "" || form[field] === undefined) continue;
      payload[field] = ["number", "capacity"].includes(field) ? Number(form[field]) : form[field];
    }
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}${editingId ? `/${editingId}` : ""}`, {
        token,
        method: editingId ? "PATCH" : "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      setNotice(editingId ? "Facility updated." : "Facility created.");
      clearForm();
      setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1);
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.message);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const archive = async (record) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Archive ${record.name || record.roomNumber || record.bedNumber || "this facility"}?`)) return;
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}/${record._id}`, { token, method: "DELETE" });
      setNotice("Facility archived.");
      setSelectedRecord(null);
      if (editingId === record._id) clearForm();
      setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1);
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.message);
    }
  };

  const view = async (record) => {
    setError("");
    try {
      const result = await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}/${record._id}`, { token });
      setSelectedRecord(result.data);
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
  };

  const relatedOptions = (field) => field === "blockId" ? parents.blocks : field === "floorId" ? parents.floors.filter((item) => !form.blockId || String(item.blockId) === String(form.blockId)) : parents.rooms;
  const labelFor = (record) => resource === "blocks" ? record.name : resource === "floors" ? (record.name || `Floor ${record.number}`) : resource === "rooms" ? record.roomNumber : record.bedNumber;
  if (showProfile) return <HostelProfile token={token} onBack={() => setShowProfile(false)} onSaved={onHostelChanged} />;

  return (
    <section className="facility-manager" aria-labelledby="facility-heading">
      <div className="facility-heading-row">
        <div><span className="dashboard-kicker">HOSTEL CONFIGURATION</span><h2 id="facility-heading">Facility Management</h2><p>Manage real blocks, floors, rooms, and beds in the selected hostel.</p></div>
        <div className="facility-heading-actions">{role === "OWNER" || (role === "SYSTEM_ADMIN" && hasTenant) ? <button type="button" className="facility-secondary" onClick={() => setShowProfile(true)}><Building2 size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Hostel profile</button> : null}<button type="button" className="facility-secondary" onClick={() => setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1)}><RefreshCw size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Refresh</button></div>
      </div>
      <div className="facility-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Facility type">
        {RESOURCES.map(({ id, label, icon: Icon }) => <button key={id} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={resource === id} className={resource === id ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => { setResource(id); clearForm(); setSelectedRecord(null); setNotice(""); setError(""); }}><Icon size={17} aria-hidden="true" />{label}</button>)}
      </div>
      <div className="facility-layout">
        <section className="facility-panel" aria-label={`${resource} records`}>
          <div className="facility-list-toolbar"><label htmlFor="facility-search">Search {resource}<input id="facility-search" type="search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search current hostel records" /></label><span>{items.length} shown</span></div>
          {loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading {resource} from the server…</p> : null}
          {!loading && !error && items.length === 0 ? <p className="facility-empty">No records yet</p> : null}
          {!loading && items.length > 0 ? <div className="facility-record-list">{items.map((record) => <article className="facility-record" key={record._id}><div><strong>{labelFor(record)}</strong><span>{resource === "floors" ? `Block ${parents.blocks.find((item) => String(item._id) === String(record.blockId))?.name || "—"}` : resource === "rooms" ? `${record.roomType || "Room"} · Capacity ${record.capacity}` : resource === "beds" ? `${parents.rooms.find((item) => String(item._id) === String(record.roomId))?.roomNumber || "Room —"} · ${record.status}` : record.status}</span></div><div className="facility-row-actions"><button type="button" onClick={() => view(record)}>View</button><button type="button" onClick={() => startEdit(record)}>Edit</button><button type="button" className="archive" onClick={() => archive(record)}>Archive</button></div></article>)}</div> : null}
          {selectedRecord ? <aside className="student-details" aria-label="Facility details"><strong>{labelFor(selectedRecord)}</strong>{Object.entries(selectedRecord).filter(([key, value]) => !["_id", "__v", "hostelId", "isDeleted", "createdAt", "updatedAt"].includes(key) && value !== null && typeof value !== "object").map(([key, value]) => <span key={key}>{key}: {String(value)}</span>)}</aside> : null}
        </section>
        <form className="facility-panel facility-form" onSubmit={submit}>
          <div className="facility-form-heading"><div><h3>{editingId ? `Edit ${resource.slice(0, -1)}` : `Add ${resource.slice(0, -1)}`}</h3><p>Changes are saved to the hostel database.</p></div>{editingId ? <button type="button" className="facility-icon-button" aria-label="Cancel edit" onClick={clearForm}><X size={17} /></button> : null}</div>
          {fields.map((field) => <label className="facility-field" key={field}><span>{LABELS[field]}</span>{["blockId", "floorId", "roomId", "status"].includes(field) ? <select required={field !== "status"} value={form[field]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value, ...(field === "blockId" ? { floorId: "" } : {}) }))}><option value="">{field === "status" ? "Use default status" : `Select ${LABELS[field].toLowerCase()}`}</option>{field === "status" ? ["AVAILABLE", "RESERVED", "MAINTENANCE"].map((status) => <option key={status} value={status}>{status}</option>) : relatedOptions(field).map((item) => <option key={item._id} value={item._id}>{field === "blockId" ? item.name : field === "floorId" ? (item.name || `Floor ${item.number}`) : item.roomNumber}</option>)}</select> : <input required={!["name"].includes(field) || resource === "blocks"} type={field === "capacity" || field === "number" ? "number" : "text"} min={field === "capacity" ? 1 : field === "number" ? -10 : undefined} max={field === "capacity" ? 500 : field === "number" ? 1000 : undefined} value={form[field]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value }))} />}</label>)}
          {error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}
          <button type="submit" className="facility-submit" disabled={saving}><Save size={16} aria-hidden="true" />{saving ? "Saving…" : editingId ? "Save changes" : <><Plus size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Add facility</>}</button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


function HostelProfile({ token, onBack, onSaved }) {
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: "", address: "", city: "", state: "", pincode: "", contact: "", email: "", photoUrl: "" });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", { token, signal: controller.signal }).then((response) => {
      setProfile(response.data);
      setForm((current) => ({ ...current, ...Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(current).map((key) => [key, response.data[key] || ""])) }));
    }).catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); }).finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token]);
  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true); setError(""); setNotice("");
    try {
      const response = await apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", { token, method: "PATCH", body: JSON.stringify(form) });
      setProfile(response.data); setNotice("Hostel profile updated."); onSaved?.();
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };
  return <section className="facility-manager" aria-labelledby="hostel-profile-heading"><div className="facility-heading-row"><div><span className="dashboard-kicker">HOSTEL SETTINGS</span><h2 id="hostel-profile-heading">Hostel Profile</h2><p>{profile?.name || "Current hostel"} · update contact and location information.</p></div><button className="facility-secondary" type="button" onClick={onBack}>Back to facilities</button></div><form className="facility-panel facility-profile-form" onSubmit={submit}>{loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading hostel profile…</p> : null}{!loading ? <><div className="hostel-identity-details"><strong>Hostel Name: {profile?.name}</strong><span>Hostel ID: {profile?._id}</span><span>Hostel Code: {profile?.hostelCode || "Not available"}</span>{profile?.accessCode ? <span>Hostel Access Code: {profile.accessCode}</span> : null}</div><PhotoField token={token} subject="hostel" label="Hostel Photo" value={form.photoUrl} onChange={(photoUrl) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, photoUrl }))} />{Object.entries({ name: "Hostel name", address: "Address", city: "City", state: "State / Union Territory", pincode: "Pincode", contact: "Contact number", email: "Email address" }).map(([key, label]) => <label className="facility-field" key={key}><span>{label}</span><input required={key === "name"} type={key === "email" ? "email" : "text"} value={form[key]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: event.target.value }))} /></label>)}</> : null}{error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}<button className="facility-submit" type="submit" disabled={loading || saving}><Save size={16} aria-hidden="true" />{saving ? "Saving…" : "Save hostel profile"}</button></form></section>;
}
export default FacilityManager;
