import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Archive, Building2, DoorOpen, Layers3, Plus, RefreshCw, Save, X } from "lucide-react";
import { apiRequest } from "../services/api.js";
import PhotoField from "../components/PhotoField.jsx";

const RESOURCES = [
  { id: "blocks", label: "Blocks", icon: Building2 },
  { id: "floors", label: "Floors", icon: Layers3 },
  { id: "rooms", label: "Rooms", icon: DoorOpen },
  { id: "beds", label: "Beds", icon: Archive }
];
const EMPTY = { name: "", blockId: "", number: "", floorId: "", roomNumber: "", roomType: "Standard", capacity: "1", roomId: "", bedNumber: "", status: "AVAILABLE" };
const LABELS = { name: "Block name", blockId: "Block", number: "Floor number", floorId: "Floor", roomNumber: "Room number", roomType: "Room type", capacity: "Bed capacity", roomId: "Room", bedNumber: "Bed number", status: "Status" };

const PUBLIC_PRICING_FIELDS = [
  { key: "singleSharing", label: "Single sharing rent / month" },
  { key: "doubleSharing", label: "Double sharing rent / month" },
  { key: "tripleSharing", label: "Triple sharing rent / month" },
  { key: "fourSharing", label: "Four sharing rent / month" },
  { key: "securityDeposit", label: "Security deposit" },
  { key: "messCharges", label: "Mess charges / month" }
];

function FacilityManager({ token, role, hasTenant, onHostelChanged }) {
  const [resource, setResource] = useState("blocks");
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [parents, setParents] = useState({ blocks: [], floors: [], rooms: [] });
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY);
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState("");
  const [showProfile, setShowProfile] = useState(false);
  const [selectedRecord, setSelectedRecord] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    Promise.all(["blocks", "floors", "rooms"].map(async (key) => {
      const result = await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${key}?limit=100`, { token, signal: controller.signal });
      return [key, result.data.items];
    })).then((records) => setParents(Object.fromEntries(records)))
      .catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token, refreshKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}?limit=100${query.trim() ? `&q=${encodeURIComponent(query.trim())}` : ""}`, { token, signal: controller.signal })
      .then((result) => setItems(result.data.items))
      .catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); })
      .finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [resource, query, token, refreshKey]);

  const fields = useMemo(() => {
    if (resource === "blocks") return ["name"];
    if (resource === "floors") return ["blockId", "number", "name"];
    if (resource === "rooms") return ["blockId", "floorId", "roomNumber", "roomType", "capacity", "status"];
    return ["roomId", "bedNumber", "status"];
  }, [resource]);

  const clearForm = () => { setForm(EMPTY); setEditingId(""); };
  const startEdit = (record) => {
    setEditingId(record._id);
    setForm({ ...EMPTY, ...record, blockId: record.blockId || "", floorId: record.floorId || "", roomId: record.roomId || "" });
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
  };

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    const payload = {};
    for (const field of fields) {
      if (form[field] === "" || form[field] === undefined) continue;
      payload[field] = ["number", "capacity"].includes(field) ? Number(form[field]) : form[field];
    }
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}${editingId ? `/${editingId}` : ""}`, {
        token,
        method: editingId ? "PATCH" : "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      setNotice(editingId ? "Facility updated." : "Facility created.");
      clearForm();
      setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1);
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.message);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const deleteFacility = async (record) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Delete ${record.name || record.roomNumber || record.bedNumber || "this facility"} from active hostel records? This is a soft delete; historical data is retained and dependent or occupied records are protected.`)) return;
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}/${record._id}`, { token, method: "DELETE" });
      setNotice("Facility deleted from active records. Historical data is retained.");
      setSelectedRecord(null);
      if (editingId === record._id) clearForm();
      setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1);
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.message);
    }
  };

  const view = async (record) => {
    setError("");
    try {
      const result = await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}/${record._id}`, { token });
      setSelectedRecord(result.data);
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
  };

  const relatedOptions = (field) => field === "blockId" ? parents.blocks : field === "floorId" ? parents.floors.filter((item) => !form.blockId || String(item.blockId) === String(form.blockId)) : parents.rooms;
  const labelFor = (record) => resource === "blocks" ? record.name : resource === "floors" ? (record.name || `Floor ${record.number}`) : resource === "rooms" ? record.roomNumber : record.bedNumber;
  if (showProfile) return <HostelProfile token={token} onBack={() => setShowProfile(false)} onSaved={onHostelChanged} />;

  return (
    <section className="facility-manager" aria-labelledby="facility-heading">
      <div className="facility-heading-row">
        <div><span className="dashboard-kicker">HOSTEL CONFIGURATION</span><h2 id="facility-heading">Facility Management</h2><p>Manage real blocks, floors, rooms, and beds in the selected hostel.</p></div>
        <div className="facility-heading-actions">{role === "OWNER" || (role === "SYSTEM_ADMIN" && hasTenant) ? <button type="button" className="facility-secondary" onClick={() => setShowProfile(true)}><Building2 size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Hostel profile</button> : null}<button type="button" className="facility-secondary" onClick={() => setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1)}><RefreshCw size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Refresh</button></div>
      </div>
      <div className="facility-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Facility type">
        {RESOURCES.map(({ id, label, icon: Icon }) => <button key={id} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={resource === id} className={resource === id ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => { setResource(id); clearForm(); setSelectedRecord(null); setNotice(""); setError(""); }}><Icon size={17} aria-hidden="true" />{label}</button>)}
      </div>
      <div className="facility-layout">
        <section className="facility-panel" aria-label={`${resource} records`}>
          <div className="facility-list-toolbar"><label htmlFor="facility-search">Search {resource}<input id="facility-search" type="search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search current hostel records" /></label><span>{items.length} shown</span></div>
          {loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading {resource} from the serverâ€¦</p> : null}
          {!loading && !error && items.length === 0 ? <p className="facility-empty">No records yet</p> : null}
          {!loading && items.length > 0 ? <div className="facility-record-list">{items.map((record) => <article className="facility-record" key={record._id}><div><strong>{labelFor(record)}</strong><span>{resource === "floors" ? `Block ${parents.blocks.find((item) => String(item._id) === String(record.blockId))?.name || "â€”"}` : resource === "rooms" ? `${record.roomType || "Room"} Â· Capacity ${record.capacity}` : resource === "beds" ? `${parents.rooms.find((item) => String(item._id) === String(record.roomId))?.roomNumber || "Room â€”"} Â· ${record.status}` : record.status}</span></div><div className="facility-row-actions"><button type="button" onClick={() => view(record)}>View</button><button type="button" onClick={() => startEdit(record)}>Edit</button><button type="button" className="archive" onClick={() => deleteFacility(record)}>Delete</button></div></article>)}</div> : null}
          {selectedRecord ? <aside className="student-details" aria-label="Facility details"><strong>{labelFor(selectedRecord)}</strong>{Object.entries(selectedRecord).filter(([key, value]) => !["_id", "__v", "hostelId", "isDeleted", "createdAt", "updatedAt"].includes(key) && value !== null && typeof value !== "object").map(([key, value]) => <span key={key}>{key}: {String(value)}</span>)}</aside> : null}
        </section>
        <form className="facility-panel facility-form" onSubmit={submit}>
          <div className="facility-form-heading"><div><h3>{editingId ? `Edit ${resource.slice(0, -1)}` : `Add ${resource.slice(0, -1)}`}</h3><p>Changes are saved to the hostel database.</p></div>{editingId ? <button type="button" className="facility-icon-button" aria-label="Cancel edit" onClick={clearForm}><X size={17} /></button> : null}</div>
          {fields.map((field) => <label className="facility-field" key={field}><span>{LABELS[field]}</span>{["blockId", "floorId", "roomId", "status"].includes(field) ? <select required={field !== "status"} value={form[field]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value, ...(field === "blockId" ? { floorId: "" } : {}) }))}><option value="">{field === "status" ? "Use default status" : `Select ${LABELS[field].toLowerCase()}`}</option>{field === "status" ? ["AVAILABLE", "RESERVED", "MAINTENANCE"].map((status) => <option key={status} value={status}>{status}</option>) : relatedOptions(field).map((item) => <option key={item._id} value={item._id}>{field === "blockId" ? item.name : field === "floorId" ? (item.name || `Floor ${item.number}`) : item.roomNumber}</option>)}</select> : <input required={!["name"].includes(field) || resource === "blocks"} type={field === "capacity" || field === "number" ? "number" : "text"} min={field === "capacity" ? 1 : field === "number" ? -10 : undefined} max={field === "capacity" ? 500 : field === "number" ? 1000 : undefined} value={form[field]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value }))} />}</label>)}
          {error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}
          <button type="submit" className="facility-submit" disabled={saving}><Save size={16} aria-hidden="true" />{saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : editingId ? "Save changes" : <><Plus size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Add facility</>}</button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


function HostelProfile({ token, onBack, onSaved }) {
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: "", address: "", city: "", state: "", pincode: "", contact: "", email: "", photoUrl: "", pricing: { singleSharing: "", doubleSharing: "", tripleSharing: "", fourSharing: "", securityDeposit: "", messCharges: "", messIncluded: "unknown" } });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", { token, signal: controller.signal }).then((response) => {
      setProfile(response.data);
      setForm({
        name: response.data.name || "",
        address: response.data.address || "",
        city: response.data.city || "",
        state: response.data.state || "",
        pincode: response.data.pincode || "",
        contact: response.data.contact || "",
        email: response.data.email || "",
        photoUrl: response.data.photoUrl || "",
        pricing: {
          singleSharing: response.data.pricing?.singleSharing ?? "",
          doubleSharing: response.data.pricing?.doubleSharing ?? "",
          tripleSharing: response.data.pricing?.tripleSharing ?? "",
          fourSharing: response.data.pricing?.fourSharing ?? "",
          securityDeposit: response.data.pricing?.securityDeposit ?? "",
          messCharges: response.data.pricing?.messCharges ?? "",
          messIncluded: response.data.pricing?.messIncluded === true
            ? "yes"
            : response.data.pricing?.messIncluded === false
              ? "no"
              : "unknown"
        }
      });
    }).catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); }).finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token]);
  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true); setError(""); setNotice("");
    try {
      const amountFields = [
        "singleSharing", "doubleSharing", "tripleSharing", "fourSharing",
        "securityDeposit", "messCharges"
      ];
      const payload = {
        ...form,
        pricing: {
          ...Object.fromEntries(amountFields.map((key) => [
            key,
            form.pricing[key] === "" || form.pricing[key] == null
              ? null
              : Number(form.pricing[key])
          ])),
          messIncluded: form.pricing.messIncluded === "yes"
            ? true
            : form.pricing.messIncluded === "no"
              ? false
              : null
        }
      };

      const response = await apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", {
        token,
        method: "PATCH",
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      setProfile(response.data); setNotice("Hostel profile updated."); onSaved?.();
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };
  return <section className="facility-manager" aria-labelledby="hostel-profile-heading"><div className="facility-heading-row"><div><span className="dashboard-kicker">HOSTEL SETTINGS</span><h2 id="hostel-profile-heading">Hostel Profile</h2><p>{profile?.name || "Current hostel"} Â· update contact and location information.</p></div><button className="facility-secondary" type="button" onClick={onBack}>Back to facilities</button></div><form className="facility-panel facility-profile-form" onSubmit={submit}>{loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading hostel profileâ€¦</p> : null}{!loading ? <><div className="hostel-identity-details"><strong>Hostel Name: {profile?.name}</strong><span>Hostel ID: {profile?._id}</span><span>Hostel Code: {profile?.hostelCode || "Not available"}</span>{profile?.accessCode ? <span>Hostel Access Code: {profile.accessCode}</span> : null}</div><PhotoField token={token} subject="hostel" label="Hostel Photo" value={form.photoUrl} onChange={(photoUrl) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, photoUrl }))} />{Object.entries({ name: "Hostel name", address: "Address", city: "City", state: "State / Union Territory", pincode: "Pincode", contact: "Contact number", email: "Email address" }).map(([key, label]) => <label className="facility-field" key={key}><span>{label}</span><input required={key === "name"} type={key === "email" ? "email" : "text"} value={form[key]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: event.target.value }))} /></label>)}{PUBLIC_PRICING_FIELDS.map(({ key, label }) => (
  <label className="facility-field" key={key}>
    <span>{label} (INR)</span>
    <input
      type="number"
      min={key === "securityDeposit" || key === "messCharges" ? 0 : 1}
      max="10000000"
      step="1"
      value={form.pricing[key]}
      onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({
        ...current,
        pricing: { ...current.pricing, [key]: event.target.value }
      }))}
    />
  </label>
))}
<label className="facility-field">
  <span>Mess included in rent?</span>
  <select
    value={form.pricing.messIncluded}
    onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      pricing: { ...current.pricing, messIncluded: event.target.value }
    }))}
  >
    <option value="unknown">Not specified</option>
    <option value="yes">Yes</option>
    <option value="no">No</option>
  </select>
</label></> : null}{error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}<button className="facility-submit" type="submit" disabled={loading || saving}><Save size={16} aria-hidden="true" />{saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : "Save hostel profile"}</button></form></section>;
}
export default FacilityManager;

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Archive, Building2, DoorOpen, Layers3, Plus, RefreshCw, Save, X } from "lucide-react";
import { apiRequest } from "../services/api.js";
import PhotoField from "../components/PhotoField.jsx";

const RESOURCES = [
  { id: "blocks", label: "Blocks", icon: Building2 },
  { id: "floors", label: "Floors", icon: Layers3 },
  { id: "rooms", label: "Rooms", icon: DoorOpen },
  { id: "beds", label: "Beds", icon: Archive }
];
const EMPTY = { name: "", blockId: "", number: "", floorId: "", roomNumber: "", roomType: "Standard", capacity: "1", roomId: "", bedNumber: "", status: "AVAILABLE" };
const LABELS = { name: "Block name", blockId: "Block", number: "Floor number", floorId: "Floor", roomNumber: "Room number", roomType: "Room type", capacity: "Bed capacity", roomId: "Room", bedNumber: "Bed number", status: "Status" };

function FacilityManager({ token, role, hasTenant, onHostelChanged }) {
  const [resource, setResource] = useState("blocks");
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [parents, setParents] = useState({ blocks: [], floors: [], rooms: [] });
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY);
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState("");
  const [showProfile, setShowProfile] = useState(false);
  const [selectedRecord, setSelectedRecord] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    Promise.all(["blocks", "floors", "rooms"].map(async (key) => {
      const result = await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${key}?limit=100`, { token, signal: controller.signal });
      return [key, result.data.items];
    })).then((records) => setParents(Object.fromEntries(records)))
      .catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token, refreshKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}?limit=100${query.trim() ? `&q=${encodeURIComponent(query.trim())}` : ""}`, { token, signal: controller.signal })
      .then((result) => setItems(result.data.items))
      .catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); })
      .finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [resource, query, token, refreshKey]);

  const fields = useMemo(() => {
    if (resource === "blocks") return ["name"];
    if (resource === "floors") return ["blockId", "number", "name"];
    if (resource === "rooms") return ["blockId", "floorId", "roomNumber", "roomType", "capacity", "status"];
    return ["roomId", "bedNumber", "status"];
  }, [resource]);

  const clearForm = () => { setForm(EMPTY); setEditingId(""); };
  const startEdit = (record) => {
    setEditingId(record._id);
    setForm({ ...EMPTY, ...record, blockId: record.blockId || "", floorId: record.floorId || "", roomId: record.roomId || "" });
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
  };

  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    const payload = {};
    for (const field of fields) {
      if (form[field] === "" || form[field] === undefined) continue;
      payload[field] = ["number", "capacity"].includes(field) ? Number(form[field]) : form[field];
    }
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}${editingId ? `/${editingId}` : ""}`, {
        token,
        method: editingId ? "PATCH" : "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      setNotice(editingId ? "Facility updated." : "Facility created.");
      clearForm();
      setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1);
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.message);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const deleteFacility = async (record) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Delete ${record.name || record.roomNumber || record.bedNumber || "this facility"} from active hostel records? This is a soft delete; historical data is retained and dependent or occupied records are protected.`)) return;
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}/${record._id}`, { token, method: "DELETE" });
      setNotice("Facility deleted from active records. Historical data is retained.");
      setSelectedRecord(null);
      if (editingId === record._id) clearForm();
      setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1);
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(requestError.message);
    }
  };

  const view = async (record) => {
    setError("");
    try {
      const result = await apiRequest(`/api/facilities/${resource}/${record._id}`, { token });
      setSelectedRecord(result.data);
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
  };

  const relatedOptions = (field) => field === "blockId" ? parents.blocks : field === "floorId" ? parents.floors.filter((item) => !form.blockId || String(item.blockId) === String(form.blockId)) : parents.rooms;
  const labelFor = (record) => resource === "blocks" ? record.name : resource === "floors" ? (record.name || `Floor ${record.number}`) : resource === "rooms" ? record.roomNumber : record.bedNumber;
  if (showProfile) return <HostelProfile token={token} onBack={() => setShowProfile(false)} onSaved={onHostelChanged} />;

  return (
    <section className="facility-manager" aria-labelledby="facility-heading">
      <div className="facility-heading-row">
        <div><span className="dashboard-kicker">HOSTEL CONFIGURATION</span><h2 id="facility-heading">Facility Management</h2><p>Manage real blocks, floors, rooms, and beds in the selected hostel.</p></div>
        <div className="facility-heading-actions">{role === "OWNER" || (role === "SYSTEM_ADMIN" && hasTenant) ? <button type="button" className="facility-secondary" onClick={() => setShowProfile(true)}><Building2 size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Hostel profile</button> : null}<button type="button" className="facility-secondary" onClick={() => setRefreshKey((key) => key + 1)}><RefreshCw size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Refresh</button></div>
      </div>
      <div className="facility-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Facility type">
        {RESOURCES.map(({ id, label, icon: Icon }) => <button key={id} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={resource === id} className={resource === id ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => { setResource(id); clearForm(); setSelectedRecord(null); setNotice(""); setError(""); }}><Icon size={17} aria-hidden="true" />{label}</button>)}
      </div>
      <div className="facility-layout">
        <section className="facility-panel" aria-label={`${resource} records`}>
          <div className="facility-list-toolbar"><label htmlFor="facility-search">Search {resource}<input id="facility-search" type="search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search current hostel records" /></label><span>{items.length} shown</span></div>
          {loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading {resource} from the serverâ€¦</p> : null}
          {!loading && !error && items.length === 0 ? <p className="facility-empty">No records yet</p> : null}
          {!loading && items.length > 0 ? <div className="facility-record-list">{items.map((record) => <article className="facility-record" key={record._id}><div><strong>{labelFor(record)}</strong><span>{resource === "floors" ? `Block ${parents.blocks.find((item) => String(item._id) === String(record.blockId))?.name || "â€”"}` : resource === "rooms" ? `${record.roomType || "Room"} Â· Capacity ${record.capacity}` : resource === "beds" ? `${parents.rooms.find((item) => String(item._id) === String(record.roomId))?.roomNumber || "Room â€”"} Â· ${record.status}` : record.status}</span></div><div className="facility-row-actions"><button type="button" onClick={() => view(record)}>View</button><button type="button" onClick={() => startEdit(record)}>Edit</button><button type="button" className="archive" onClick={() => deleteFacility(record)}>Delete</button></div></article>)}</div> : null}
          {selectedRecord ? <aside className="student-details" aria-label="Facility details"><strong>{labelFor(selectedRecord)}</strong>{Object.entries(selectedRecord).filter(([key, value]) => !["_id", "__v", "hostelId", "isDeleted", "createdAt", "updatedAt"].includes(key) && value !== null && typeof value !== "object").map(([key, value]) => <span key={key}>{key}: {String(value)}</span>)}</aside> : null}
        </section>
        <form className="facility-panel facility-form" onSubmit={submit}>
          <div className="facility-form-heading"><div><h3>{editingId ? `Edit ${resource.slice(0, -1)}` : `Add ${resource.slice(0, -1)}`}</h3><p>Changes are saved to the hostel database.</p></div>{editingId ? <button type="button" className="facility-icon-button" aria-label="Cancel edit" onClick={clearForm}><X size={17} /></button> : null}</div>
          {fields.map((field) => <label className="facility-field" key={field}><span>{LABELS[field]}</span>{["blockId", "floorId", "roomId", "status"].includes(field) ? <select required={field !== "status"} value={form[field]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value, ...(field === "blockId" ? { floorId: "" } : {}) }))}><option value="">{field === "status" ? "Use default status" : `Select ${LABELS[field].toLowerCase()}`}</option>{field === "status" ? ["AVAILABLE", "RESERVED", "MAINTENANCE"].map((status) => <option key={status} value={status}>{status}</option>) : relatedOptions(field).map((item) => <option key={item._id} value={item._id}>{field === "blockId" ? item.name : field === "floorId" ? (item.name || `Floor ${item.number}`) : item.roomNumber}</option>)}</select> : <input required={!["name"].includes(field) || resource === "blocks"} type={field === "capacity" || field === "number" ? "number" : "text"} min={field === "capacity" ? 1 : field === "number" ? -10 : undefined} max={field === "capacity" ? 500 : field === "number" ? 1000 : undefined} value={form[field]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value }))} />}</label>)}
          {error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}
          <button type="submit" className="facility-submit" disabled={saving}><Save size={16} aria-hidden="true" />{saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : editingId ? "Save changes" : <><Plus size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Add facility</>}</button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


function HostelProfile({ token, onBack, onSaved }) {
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: "", address: "", city: "", state: "", pincode: "", contact: "", email: "", photoUrl: "" });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", { token, signal: controller.signal }).then((response) => {
      setProfile(response.data);
      setForm((current) => ({ ...current, ...Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(current).map((key) => [key, response.data[key] || ""])) }));
    }).catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); }).finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token]);
  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true); setError(""); setNotice("");
    try {
      const response = await apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", { token, method: "PATCH", body: JSON.stringify(form) });
      setProfile(response.data); setNotice("Hostel profile updated."); onSaved?.();
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };
  return <section className="facility-manager" aria-labelledby="hostel-profile-heading"><div className="facility-heading-row"><div><span className="dashboard-kicker">HOSTEL SETTINGS</span><h2 id="hostel-profile-heading">Hostel Profile</h2><p>{profile?.name || "Current hostel"} Â· update contact and location information.</p></div><button className="facility-secondary" type="button" onClick={onBack}>Back to facilities</button></div><form className="facility-panel facility-profile-form" onSubmit={submit}>{loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading hostel profileâ€¦</p> : null}{!loading ? <><div className="hostel-identity-details"><strong>Hostel Name: {profile?.name}</strong><span>Hostel ID: {profile?._id}</span><span>Hostel Code: {profile?.hostelCode || "Not available"}</span>{profile?.accessCode ? <span>Hostel Access Code: {profile.accessCode}</span> : null}</div><PhotoField token={token} subject="hostel" label="Hostel Photo" value={form.photoUrl} onChange={(photoUrl) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, photoUrl }))} />{Object.entries({ name: "Hostel name", address: "Address", city: "City", state: "State / Union Territory", pincode: "Pincode", contact: "Contact number", email: "Email address" }).map(([key, label]) => <label className="facility-field" key={key}><span>{label}</span><input required={key === "name"} type={key === "email" ? "email" : "text"} value={form[key]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: event.target.value }))} /></label>)}</> : null}{error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}<button className="facility-submit" type="submit" disabled={loading || saving}><Save size={16} aria-hidden="true" />{saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : "Save hostel profile"}</button></form></section>;
}
export default FacilityManager;

        {RESOURCES.map(({ id, label, icon: Icon }) => <button key={id} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={resource === id} className={resource === id ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => { setResource(id); clearForm(); setSelectedRecord(null); setNotice(""); setError(""); }}><Icon size={17} aria-hidden="true" />{label}</button>)}
      </div>
      <div className="facility-layout">
        <section className="facility-panel" aria-label={`${resource} records`}>
          <div className="facility-list-toolbar"><label htmlFor="facility-search">Search {resource}<input id="facility-search" type="search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search current hostel records" /></label><span>{items.length} shown</span></div>

          {!loading && !error && items.length === 0 ? <p className="facility-empty">No records yet</p> : null}
          {!loading && items.length > 0 ? <div className="facility-record-list">{items.map((record) => <article className="facility-record" key={record._id}><div><strong>{labelFor(record)}</strong><span>{resource === "floors" ? `Block ${parents.blocks.find((item) => String(item._id) === String(record.blockId))?.name || "â€”"}` : resource === "rooms" ? `${record.roomType || "Room"} Â· Capacity ${record.capacity}` : resource === "beds" ? `${parents.rooms.find((item) => String(item._id) === String(record.roomId))?.roomNumber || "Room â€”"} Â· ${record.status}` : record.status}</span></div><div className="facility-row-actions"><button type="button" onClick={() => view(record)}>View</button><button type="button" onClick={() => startEdit(record)}>Edit</button><button type="button" className="archive" onClick={() => deleteFacility(record)}>Delete</button></div></article>)}</div> : null}
          {selectedRecord ? <aside className="student-details" aria-label="Facility details"><strong>{labelFor(selectedRecord)}</strong>{Object.entries(selectedRecord).filter(([key, value]) => !["_id", "__v", "hostelId", "isDeleted", "createdAt", "updatedAt"].includes(key) && value !== null && typeof value !== "object").map(([key, value]) => <span key={key}>{key}: {String(value)}</span>)}</aside> : null}
        </section>
        <form className="facility-panel facility-form" onSubmit={submit}>
          <div className="facility-form-heading"><div><h3>{editingId ? `Edit ${resource.slice(0, -1)}` : `Add ${resource.slice(0, -1)}`}</h3><p>Changes are saved to the hostel database.</p></div>{editingId ? <button type="button" className="facility-icon-button" aria-label="Cancel edit" onClick={clearForm}><X size={17} /></button> : null}</div>
          {fields.map((field) => <label className="facility-field" key={field}><span>{LABELS[field]}</span>{["blockId", "floorId", "roomId", "status"].includes(field) ? <select required={field !== "status"} value={form[field]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value, ...(field === "blockId" ? { floorId: "" } : {}) }))}><option value="">{field === "status" ? "Use default status" : `Select ${LABELS[field].toLowerCase()}`}</option>{field === "status" ? ["AVAILABLE", "RESERVED", "MAINTENANCE"].map((status) => <option key={status} value={status}>{status}</option>) : relatedOptions(field).map((item) => <option key={item._id} value={item._id}>{field === "blockId" ? item.name : field === "floorId" ? (item.name || `Floor ${item.number}`) : item.roomNumber}</option>)}</select> : <input required={!["name"].includes(field) || resource === "blocks"} type={field === "capacity" || field === "number" ? "number" : "text"} min={field === "capacity" ? 1 : field === "number" ? -10 : undefined} max={field === "capacity" ? 500 : field === "number" ? 1000 : undefined} value={form[field]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value }))} />}</label>)}
          {error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}
          <button type="submit" className="facility-submit" disabled={saving}><Save size={16} aria-hidden="true" />{saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : editingId ? "Save changes" : <><Plus size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> Add facility</>}</button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


function HostelProfile({ token, onBack, onSaved }) {
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: "", address: "", city: "", state: "", pincode: "", contact: "", email: "", photoUrl: "" });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", { token, signal: controller.signal }).then((response) => {
      setProfile(response.data);
      setForm((current) => ({ ...current, ...Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(current).map((key) => [key, response.data[key] || ""])) }));
    }).catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); }).finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token]);
  const submit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true); setError(""); setNotice("");
    try {
      const response = await apiRequest("/api/hostels/current", { token, method: "PATCH", body: JSON.stringify(form) });
      setProfile(response.data); setNotice("Hostel profile updated."); onSaved?.();
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };
  return <section className="facility-manager" aria-labelledby="hostel-profile-heading"><div className="facility-heading-row"><div><span className="dashboard-kicker">HOSTEL SETTINGS</span><h2 id="hostel-profile-heading">Hostel Profile</h2><p>{profile?.name || "Current hostel"} Â· update contact and location information.</p></div><button className="facility-secondary" type="button" onClick={onBack}>Back to facilities</button></div><form className="facility-panel facility-profile-form" onSubmit={submit}>{loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading hostel profileâ€¦</p> : null}{!loading ? <><div className="hostel-identity-details"><strong>Hostel Name: {profile?.name}</strong><span>Hostel ID: {profile?._id}</span><span>Hostel Code: {profile?.hostelCode || "Not available"}</span>{profile?.accessCode ? <span>Hostel Access Code: {profile.accessCode}</span> : null}</div><PhotoField token={token} subject="hostel" label="Hostel Photo" value={form.photoUrl} onChange={(photoUrl) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, photoUrl }))} />{Object.entries({ name: "Hostel name", address: "Address", city: "City", state: "State / Union Territory", pincode: "Pincode", contact: "Contact number", email: "Email address" }).map(([key, label]) => <label className="facility-field" key={key}><span>{label}</span><input required={key === "name"} type={key === "email" ? "email" : "text"} value={form[key]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: event.target.value }))} /></label>)}</> : null}{error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}{notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}<button className="facility-submit" type="submit" disabled={loading || saving}><Save size={16} aria-hidden="true" />{saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : "Save hostel profile"}</button></form></section>;
}
export default FacilityManager;

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Building2, Eye, Pencil, Plus, RefreshCw, Save } from "lucide-react";
import { apiRequest } from "../services/api.js";

const EMPTY_FORM = { name: "", address: "", city: "", state: "", pincode: "", contact: "", email: "", photoUrl: "" };

function HostelManagement({ token, onAdd }) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [showArchived, setShowArchived] = useState(false);
  const [reload, setReload] = useState(0);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const params = new URLSearchParams({ limit: "100" });
    if (query.trim()) params.set("q", query.trim());
    if (showArchived) params.set("archived", "true");
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    apiRequest(`/api/hostels?${params}`, { token, signal: controller.signal })
      .then((result) => setItems(result.data.items))
      .catch((requestError) => { if (requestError.name !== "AbortError") setError(requestError.message); })
      .finally(() => { if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false); });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [token, query, showArchived, reload]);

  const view = async (hostel) => {
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    try {
      const result = await apiRequest(`/api/hostels/${hostel._id}`, { token });
      setSelected(result.data);
      setForm(Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(EMPTY_FORM).map((key) => [key, result.data[key] || ""])));
      setEditing(false);
      return true;
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); return false; }
  };

  const save = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!selected || saving) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    try {
      const result = await apiRequest(`/api/hostels/${selected._id}`, {
        token, method: "PATCH", body: JSON.stringify(form)
      });
      setSelected(result.data);
      setNotice("Hostel details updated.");
      setEditing(false);
      setReload((value) => value + 1);
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
    finally { setSaving(false); }
  };

  const archive = async (hostel) => {
    if (!window.confirm(`Archive ${hostel.name}? This is allowed only when active hostel resources have been archived. Historical records are retained.`)) return;
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/api/hostels/${hostel._id}`, { token, method: "DELETE" });
      setNotice("Hostel archived; its historical data was retained.");
      setSelected(null);
      setReload((value) => value + 1);
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
  };

  const restore = async (hostel) => {
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    try {
      await apiRequest(`/api/hostels/${hostel._id}/restore`, { token, method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({}) });
      setNotice("Hostel restored.");
      setReload((value) => value + 1);
    } catch (requestError) { setError(requestError.message); }
  };

  return <section className="facility-manager" aria-labelledby="hostels-heading">
    <div className="facility-heading-row">
      <div><span className="dashboard-kicker">PLATFORM ADMINISTRATION</span><h2 id="hostels-heading">Hostels</h2><p>View, edit, archive, and restore real hostel records.</p></div>
      <div className="facility-heading-actions">
        <button type="button" className="facility-secondary" onClick={onAdd}><Plus size={16} /> Add hostel</button>
        <button type="button" className="facility-secondary" onClick={() => setReload((value) => value + 1)}><RefreshCw size={16} /> Refresh</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    {error ? <p className="facility-error" role="alert">{error}</p> : null}
    {notice ? <p className="facility-notice" role="status">{notice}</p> : null}
    <div className="allocation-grid">
      <section className="facility-panel" aria-label="Hostel records">
        <div className="student-filter-row">
          <label><Building2 size={16} /><input aria-label="Search hostels" type="search" value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search name, code, city, state" /></label>
          <button type="button" className="facility-secondary" aria-pressed={showArchived} onClick={() => { setSelected(null); setShowArchived((value) => !value); }}>{showArchived ? "Show active hostels" : "Show archived"}</button>
        </div>
        {loading ? <p className="facility-state" role="status">Loading hostelsâ€¦</p> : null}
        {!loading && !items.length ? <p className="facility-empty">No records yet</p> : null}
        <div className="facility-record-list">{items.map((hostel) => <article className="facility-record" key={hostel._id}>
          <div><strong>{hostel.name}</strong><span>{hostel.hostelCode} Â· {hostel.city || "City unavailable"} Â· {hostel.status}</span></div>
          <div className="facility-row-actions">
            <button type="button" onClick={() => view(hostel)}><Eye size={14} /> View</button>
            {showArchived ? <button type="button" onClick={() => restore(hostel)}>Restore</button> : <>
              <button type="button" onClick={async () => { if (await view(hostel)) setEditing(true); }}><Pencil size={14} /> Edit</button>
              <button type="button" className="archive" onClick={() => archive(hostel)}>Archive</button>
            </>}
          </div>
        </article>)}</div>
      </section>
      {selected ? <section className="facility-panel">
        <div className="facility-form-heading"><div><h3>{editing ? "Edit hostel" : selected.name}</h3><p>{selected.hostelCode} Â· {selected.status}</p></div>
          {!editing && !showArchived ? <button type="button" className="facility-secondary" onClick={() => setEditing(true)}><Pencil size={14} /> Edit</button> : null}
        </div>
        {editing ? <form className="facility-form" onSubmit={save}>{Object.entries({ name: "Hostel name", address: "Address", city: "City", state: "State / Union Territory", pincode: "Pincode", contact: "Contact number", email: "Email address", photoUrl: "Cover photo URL" }).map(([key, label]) => <label className="facility-field" key={key}><span>{label}</span><input required={key === "name"} type={key === "email" ? "email" : key === "photoUrl" ? "url" : "text"} value={form[key]} onChange={(event) => setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: event.target.value }))} /></label>)}<button type="submit" className="facility-submit" disabled={saving}><Save size={16} />{saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : "Save changes"}</button></form>
          : <div className="student-details"><strong>{selected.name}</strong><span>Address: {selected.address || "Not provided"}</span><span>Location: {[selected.city, selected.state, selected.pincode].filter(Boolean).join(", ") || "Not provided"}</span><span>Contact: {selected.contact || "Not provided"}</span><span>Email: {selected.email || "Not provided"}</span></div>}
      </section> : null}
    </div>
  </section>;
}

export default HostelManagement;

export const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || "";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(message, status) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.status = status;
  }
}

export async function apiRequest(path, { token, signal, ...options } = {}) {
  const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});

  if (options.body !== undefined && !(options.body instanceof FormData) && !headers.has("Content-Type")) {
    headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
  }

  if (token) {
    headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${token}`);

    try {
      const selectedHostelId = window.sessionStorage.getItem("shms.selectedHostelId");

      if (selectedHostelId && !headers.has("X-Hostel-Id")) {
        headers.set("X-Hostel-Id", selectedHostelId);
      }
    } catch {
      // Backend will use the hostel context already verified in the access token.
    }
  }

  let response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${path}`, {
      ...options,
      headers,
      signal
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.name === "AbortError") {
      throw error;
    }
    if (error instanceof TypeError) {
      throw new ApiError("Unable to connect to the server. Check your connection and try again.", 0);
    }
    throw error;
  }

  const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new ApiError(payload?.message || "The request could not be completed.", response.status);
  }

  return payload;
}

export const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || "";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(message, status) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.status = status;
  }
}

export async function apiRequest(path, { token, signal, ...options } = {}) {
  const headers = new Headers(options.headers || {});

  if (options.body !== undefined && !(options.body instanceof FormData) && !headers.has("Content-Type")) {
    headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
  }

  if (token) {
    headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${token}`);
  }

  let response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${path}`, {
      ...options,
      headers,
      signal
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.name === "AbortError") {
      throw error;
    }
    if (error instanceof TypeError) {
      throw new ApiError("Unable to connect to the server. Check your connection and try again.", 0);
    }
    throw error;
  }

  const payload = await response.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new ApiError(payload?.message || "The request could not be completed.", response.status);
  }

  return payload;
}

FILE: .\server\src\app.js

const { randomUUID } = require("node:crypto");
const cors = require("cors");
const express = require("express");
const helmet = require("helmet");

const config = require("./config");
const authRoutes = require("./routes/authRoutes");
const dashboardRoutes = require("./routes/dashboardRoutes");
const facilityRoutes = require("./routes/facilityRoutes");
const hostelRoutes = require("./routes/hostelRoutes");
const studentRoutes = require("./routes/studentRoutes");
const parentRoutes = require("./routes/parentRoutes");
const allocationRoutes = require("./routes/allocationRoutes");
const staffRoutes = require("./routes/staffRoutes");
const uploadRoutes = require("./routes/uploadRoutes");
const gateRoutes = require("./routes/gateRoutes");
const paymentRoutes = require("./routes/paymentRoutes");
const outpassRoutes = require("./routes/outpassRoutes");
const complaintRoutes = require("./routes/complaintRoutes");
const helpContactRoutes = require("./routes/helpContactRoutes");
const noticeRoutes = require("./routes/noticeRoutes");
const notificationRoutes = require("./routes/notificationRoutes");

const app = express();

app.disable("x-powered-by");

app.use(helmet());

app.use(cors({
    origin: config.corsOrigin
}));

app.use((req, res, next) => {
    const requestId = randomUUID();

    req.requestId = requestId;
    res.setHeader("X-Request-ID", requestId);

    next();
});

app.use((req, res, next) => {
    const startTime = process.hrtime.bigint();

    res.on("finish", () => {
        const endTime = process.hrtime.bigint();
        const durationMs = Number(endTime - startTime) / 1_000_000;

        console.log(
            `[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${req.requestId} ${req.method} ${req.originalUrl} ${res.statusCode} ${durationMs.toFixed(2)}ms`
        );
    });

    next();
});

app.use(express.json({
    limit: config.jsonBodyLimit
}));
app.get("/health", (req, res) => {
    res.status(200).json({
        status: "ok"
    });
});

app.use("/api/auth", authRoutes);
app.use("/api/dashboard", dashboardRoutes);
app.use("/api/facilities", facilityRoutes);
app.use("/api/hostels", hostelRoutes);
app.use("/api/students", studentRoutes);
app.use("/api/parents", parentRoutes);
app.use("/api/allocations", allocationRoutes);
app.use("/api/staff", staffRoutes);
app.use("/api/uploads", uploadRoutes);
app.use("/api/gate", gateRoutes);
app.use("/api/payments", paymentRoutes);
app.use("/api/outpasses", outpassRoutes);
app.use("/api/complaints", complaintRoutes);
app.use("/api/help-contacts", helpContactRoutes);
app.use("/api/notices", noticeRoutes);
app.use("/api/notifications", notificationRoutes);

app.use((req, res) => {
    res.status(404).json({
        status: "error",
        message: "Route not found"
    });
});

app.use((err, req, res, next) => {
    if (err.type === "entity.too.large") {
        res.status(413).json({
            status: "error",
            message: "Request body too large"
        });
        return;
    }

    console.error(err);

    res.status(500).json({
        status: "error",
        message: "Internal server error"
    });
});

module.exports = app;


======================================================================
FILE: .\server\src\controllers\allocationController.js
======================================================================
const service = require("../services/allocationService");
const sendError = (error, res, next) => {
    if (error.statusCode) return res.status(error.statusCode).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
    return next(error);
};
const handler = (operation, status = 200) => async (req, res, next) => {
    try { return res.status(status).json({ status: "success", data: await operation(req) }); }
    catch (error) { return sendError(error, res, next); }
};
module.exports = {
    options: handler(service.options),
    list: handler(service.list),
    availableBeds: handler(service.listAvailableBeds),
    assign: handler(service.assign, 201),
    release: handler(service.release),
    transfer: handler(service.transfer)
};

const mongoose = require("mongoose");

const User = require("../models/User");
const Hostel = require("../models/Hostel");
const HostelMembership = require("../models/HostelMembership");
const Student = require("../models/Student");
const ROLES = require("../constants/roles");
const config = require("../config");
const { hashPassword, verifyPassword } = require("../utils/password");
const { signAccessToken } = require("../utils/token");

const invalidCredentials = (res) => res.status(401).json({
    status: "error",
    message: "Invalid credentials"
});

const conflict = (res, message) => res.status(409).json({
    status: "error",
    message
});

const asString = (value, maxLength = 256) => {
    if (typeof value !== "string") {
        return null;
    }

    const trimmed = value.trim();
    if (!trimmed || trimmed.length > maxLength) {
        return null;
    }

    return trimmed;
};

const publicUser = (user, hostelId = null, studentId = null) => ({
    id: String(user._id),
    name: user.name,
    photoUrl: user.photoUrl || "",
    email: user.email || "",
    mobile: user.mobile || "",
    role: user.role,
    hostelId,
    studentId
});

const normalizeRegistrationPayload = (body) => {
    const name = asString(body?.name, 128);
    const email = asString(body?.email, 254);
    const mobile = asString(body?.mobile, 32);
    const password = typeof body?.password === "string" ? body.password : "";
    const hostelName = asString(body?.hostelName, 128);
    const hostelCode = asString(body?.hostelCode, 64);
    const accessCode = asString(body?.accessCode, 64);
    const contact = asString(body?.contact, 64);
    const city = asString(body?.city, 128);
    const state = asString(body?.state, 128);
    const address = asString(body?.address, 512);
    const pincode = asString(body?.pincode, 24);
    const photoUrl = body?.photoUrl === undefined || body.photoUrl === ""
        ? ""
        : asString(body.photoUrl, 2048);
    const galleryImages = body?.galleryImages === undefined
        ? []
        : Array.isArray(body.galleryImages) && body.galleryImages.length <= 8
            ? body.galleryImages.map((value) => asString(value, 2048))
            : null;
    const totalRooms = body?.totalRooms === undefined || body.totalRooms === "" ? 0 : Number(body.totalRooms);
    const totalBeds = body?.totalBeds === undefined || body.totalBeds === "" ? 0 : Number(body.totalBeds);
    const bedsPerRoom = body?.bedsPerRoom === undefined || body.bedsPerRoom === "" ? 0 : Number(body.bedsPerRoom);
    const roomTypes = Array.isArray(body?.roomTypes)
        ? body.roomTypes
            .filter((value) => typeof value === "string")
            .map((value) => value.trim())
            .filter(Boolean)
            .slice(0, 8)
        : [];

    const validEmail = email && /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email);
    const mobileDigits = mobile ? mobile.replace(/\D/g, "").length : 0;
    const validMobile = mobile &&
        /^\+?[0-9][0-9\s()-]{5,30}$/.test(mobile) &&
        mobileDigits >= 7 &&
        mobileDigits <= 15;
    const contactDigits = contact ? contact.replace(/\D/g, "").length : 0;
    const validContact = !body?.contact || (
        contact &&
        /^\+?[0-9][0-9\s()-]{5,30}$/.test(contact) &&
        contactDigits >= 7 &&
        contactDigits <= 15
    );
    const validHostelEmail = !body?.hostelEmail || (
        typeof body.hostelEmail === "string" &&
        /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(body.hostelEmail.trim())
    );
    const validImageUrl = (value) => {
        if (value === "") {
            return true;
        }
        if (typeof value !== "string") {
            return false;
        }
        try {
            const url = new URL(value);
            return url.protocol === "https:" || url.protocol === "http:";
        } catch {
            return false;
        }
    };
    const validRoomSetup = [totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom].every(
        (value) => Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0 && value <= 100000
    );

    if (
        !name || !validEmail || !validMobile ||
        password.length < 8 || password.length > 1024 ||
        !hostelName || !hostelCode || !accessCode ||
        !/^[A-Z0-9_-]{3,64}$/.test(hostelCode.toUpperCase()) ||
        !/^[A-Z0-9_-]{4,64}$/.test(accessCode.toUpperCase()) ||
        !validHostelEmail || !validContact || !validImageUrl(photoUrl) ||
        !Array.isArray(galleryImages) || galleryImages.some((value) => !value || !validImageUrl(value)) ||
        !validRoomSetup
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    const normalizedRoomSetup = {
        totalRooms,
        totalBeds,
        bedsPerRoom,
        roomTypes
    };

    return {
        name,
        email: email.toLowerCase(),
        mobile,
        password,
        hostelName,
        hostelCode: hostelCode.toUpperCase(),
        accessCode: accessCode.toUpperCase(),
        contact: contact || mobile,
        city: city || "",
        state: state || "",
        address: address || "",
        pincode: pincode || "",
        photoUrl: photoUrl || "",
        galleryImages,
        roomSetup: normalizedRoomSetup,
        hostelEmail: typeof body?.hostelEmail === "string" ? body.hostelEmail.trim().toLowerCase() : ""
    };
};

const registerOwner = async (req, res, next) => {
    const payload = normalizeRegistrationPayload(req.body || {});
    if (!payload) {
        return res.status(400).json({
            status: "error",
            message: "Provide valid owner profile details plus hostel registration details. Password must be at least 8 characters."
        });
    }

    const session = await mongoose.startSession();

    try {
        let createdOwner;
        let createdHostel;

        await session.withTransaction(async () => {
            const ownerExists = await User.findOne({
                email: payload.email,
                isDeleted: { $ne: true }
            }, null, { session });
            if (ownerExists) {
                throw Object.assign(new Error("An account with that email already exists."), { statusCode: 409 });
            }

            const mobileTaken = await User.findOne({
                mobile: payload.mobile,
                isDeleted: { $ne: true }
            }, null, { session });
            if (mobileTaken) {
                throw Object.assign(new Error("An account with that mobile number already exists."), { statusCode: 409 });
            }

            const hostelCodeTaken = await Hostel.findOne({
                hostelCode: payload.hostelCode,
                isDeleted: { $ne: true }
            }, null, { session });
            if (hostelCodeTaken) {
                throw Object.assign(new Error("That hostel code is already in use."), { statusCode: 409 });
            }

            const accessCodeTaken = await Hostel.findOne({
                accessCode: payload.accessCode,
                isDeleted: { $ne: true }
            }, null, { session });
            if (accessCodeTaken) {
                throw Object.assign(new Error("That hostel access code is already in use."), { statusCode: 409 });
            }

            const passwordHash = await hashPassword(payload.password);

            createdOwner = new User({
                name: payload.name,
                email: payload.email,
                mobile: payload.mobile,
                passwordHash,
                role: ROLES.OWNER,
                status: "ACTIVE",
                hostelId: null,
                isDeleted: false
            });

            await createdOwner.save({ session });

            createdHostel = new Hostel({
                hostelCode: payload.hostelCode,
                accessCode: payload.accessCode,
                name: payload.hostelName,
                photoUrl: payload.photoUrl || "",
                galleryImages: payload.galleryImages || [],
                address: payload.address,
                city: payload.city,
                state: payload.state,
                pincode: payload.pincode,
                contact: payload.contact,
                email: payload.hostelEmail,
                roomSetup: payload.roomSetup,
                ownerId: createdOwner._id,
                status: "ACTIVE",
                isDeleted: false
            });

            await createdHostel.save({ session });

            createdOwner.hostelId = createdHostel._id;
            await createdOwner.save({ session });

            const membership = new HostelMembership({
                userId: createdOwner._id,
                hostelId: createdHostel._id,
                role: ROLES.OWNER,
                status: "ACTIVE",
                assignedBy: createdOwner._id,
                isDeleted: false
            });

            await membership.save({ session });
        });

        const account = publicUser(createdOwner, String(createdHostel._id));
        const token = signAccessToken(account, config.jwtSecret);

        return res.status(201).json({
            status: "success",
            data: {
                token,
                user: account,
                hostel: {
                    id: String(createdHostel._id),
                    hostelCode: createdHostel.hostelCode,
                    accessCode: createdHostel.accessCode,
                    name: createdHostel.name
                }
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error?.statusCode === 409) {
            return conflict(res, error.message);
        }

        if (error?.code === 11000) {
            return conflict(res, "A conflicting hostel or account record already exists.");
        }

        return next(error);
    } finally {
        await session.endSession();
    }
};

const dashboardService = require("../services/dashboardService");

const getSummary = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const rawRangeDays = req.query?.rangeDays;
        let rangeDays = 7;

        if (rawRangeDays !== undefined) {
            if (rawRangeDays !== "7" && rawRangeDays !== "30") {
                res.status(400).json({
                    status: "error",
                    message: "rangeDays must be 7 or 30."
                });
                return;
            }

            rangeDays = Number(rawRangeDays);
        }

        const summary = await dashboardService.getDashboardSummary(
            req.tenant,
            { rangeDays }
        );

        res.status(200).json({
            status: "success",
            data: summary
        });
    } catch (error) {
        next(error);
    }
};

module.exports = {
    getSummary
};

const service = require("../services/facilityService");

const respond = (operation, statusCode = 200) => async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const data = await operation(req.params.resource, req);
        res.status(statusCode).json({ status: "success", data });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error.statusCode) {
            res.status(error.statusCode).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
            return;
        }
        next(error);
    }
};

const controller = {
    list: respond(service.list),
    get: respond(service.get),
    create: respond(service.create, 201),
    update: respond(service.update),
    archive: respond(service.archive)
};

module.exports = controller;

const Hostel = require("../models/Hostel");

const Bed = require("../models/Bed");

const SAFE_FIELDS = "name hostelCode photoUrl galleryImages address city state pincode contact email roomSetup pricing paymentSettings status createdAt updatedAt";
const httpError = (statusCode, message) => Object.assign(new Error(message), { statusCode });
const safeHostel = (hostel) => {
    const record = typeof hostel.toObject === "function" ? hostel.toObject() : { ...hostel };
    delete record.accessCode;
    delete record.ownerId;
    return record;
};
const isImageUrl = (value) => {
    if (!value) return true;
    try {
        const url = new URL(value);
        return url.protocol === "http:" || url.protocol === "https:";
    } catch {
        return false;
    }
};

const getCurrent = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const fields = req.auth?.role === "OWNER" ? `${SAFE_FIELDS} accessCode` : SAFE_FIELDS;
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne(hostelScope(req, { isDeleted: { $ne: true } })).select(fields).lean();
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        return res.status(200).json({
            status: "success",
            data: { ...hostel, ...(req.auth?.role === "OWNER" ? {} : { accessCode: undefined }) }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }

const updateCurrent = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const allowed = ["name", "photoUrl", "galleryImages", "address", "city", "state", "pincode", "contact", "email", "roomSetup", "pricing", "paymentSettings"];
        const body = req.body;
        if (!body || typeof body !== "object" || Array.isArray(body)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "A JSON object is required." });
        const unknown = Object.keys(body).filter((key) => !allowed.includes(key));
        if (unknown.length) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `Unsupported field: ${unknown[0]}.` });
        if (!Object.keys(body).length) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Provide at least one hostel field to update." });
        const update = {};
        for (const field of allowed) {
            if (!Object.hasOwn(body, field)) continue;
            const value = body[field];
            if (["galleryImages"].includes(field)) {
                if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length > 8 || value.some((url) => typeof url !== "string" || url.length > 2048 || !isImageUrl(url))) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "galleryImages must contain up to 8 valid HTTP(S) image URLs." });
                }
                update[field] = value.map((url) => url.trim());
            } else if (field === "photoUrl") {
                if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > 2048 || !(isImageUrl(value) || /^\/api\/uploads\/photos\/[a-f\d-]{36}\.(?:jpg|png|webp)$/i.test(value))) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "photoUrl must be an uploaded photo reference or valid HTTP(S) URL." });
                update[field] = value.trim();
            } else if (field === "paymentSettings") {
                if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "paymentSettings must be an object." });
                }

                const unknownSettings = Object.keys(value).find(
                    (key) => !["upiId", "payeeName"].includes(key)
                );
                if (unknownSettings) {
                    return res.status(400).json({
                        status: "error",
                        message: `Unsupported payment setting: ${unknownSettings}.`
                    });
                }

                const upiId = typeof value.upiId === "string" ? value.upiId.trim() : "";
                const payeeName = typeof value.payeeName === "string" ? value.payeeName.trim() : "";

                if (upiId.length > 120 || (upiId && !/^[A-Za-z0-9._-]{2,100}@[A-Za-z0-9.-]{2,100}$/.test(upiId))) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Enter a valid UPI ID, or leave it blank." });
                }
                if (payeeName.length > 100) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Payee name must be 100 characters or shorter." });
                }

                update[field] = { upiId, payeeName };
            } else if (field === "pricing") {
                if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "pricing must be an object." });
                }

                const amountFields = [
                    "singleSharing", "doubleSharing", "tripleSharing", "fourSharing",
                    "securityDeposit", "messCharges"
                ];
                const supportedFields = [...amountFields, "messIncluded"];
                const unknownField = Object.keys(value).find((key) => !supportedFields.includes(key));

                if (unknownField) {
                    return res.status(400).json({
                        status: "error",
                        message: `Unsupported pricing field: ${unknownField}.`
                    });
                }

                const cleaned = {};

                for (const key of amountFields) {
                    const amount = value[key];
                    if (amount === undefined || amount === null || amount === "") {
                        cleaned[key] = null;
                        continue;
                    }

                    const minimum = ["securityDeposit", "messCharges"].includes(key) ? 0 : 1;
                    if (typeof amount !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount < minimum || amount > 10000000) {
                        return res.status(400).json({
                            status: "error",
                            message: `${key} must be a valid INR amount.`
                        });
                    }
                    cleaned[key] = amount;
                }

                if (
                    value.messIncluded !== undefined &&
                    value.messIncluded !== null &&
                    typeof value.messIncluded !== "boolean"
                ) {
                    return res.status(400).json({
                        status: "error",
                        message: "messIncluded must be true, false, or null."
                    });
                }

                cleaned.messIncluded = value.messIncluded ?? null;
                update[field] = cleaned;
            } else if (field === "roomSetup") {
                if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "roomSetup must be an object." });
                const { totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom, roomTypes } = value;
                if (![totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom].every((number) => Number.isInteger(number) && number >= 0 && number <= 100000) || !Array.isArray(roomTypes) || roomTypes.length > 8 || roomTypes.some((type) => typeof type !== "string" || !type.trim() || type.length > 64)) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "roomSetup contains invalid capacity or room type values." });
                }
                update[field] = { totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom, roomTypes: roomTypes.map((type) => type.trim()) };
            } else {
                const maxLength = field === "address" ? 512 : 128;
                if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim().length > maxLength) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `${field} must be a string of at most ${maxLength} characters.` });
                if (["name"].includes(field) && !value.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `${field} cannot be empty.` });
                if (field === "email" && value.trim() && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(value.trim())) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "email must be a valid email address." });
                update[field] = value.trim();
            }
        }
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne(hostelScope(req, { isDeleted: { $ne: true } }));
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        hostel.set(update);
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: hostel.toObject({ transform: (doc, ret) => { delete ret.accessCode; delete ret.ownerId; return ret; } }) });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error.statusCode) return res.status(error.statusCode).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
        if (error.name === "ValidationError") return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
        return next(error);
    }
};

const updateById = async (req, res, next) => {

    req.tenant = { hostelId: req.params.id };

const listPublic = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const page = Number(req.query.page || 1);
        const limit = Number(req.query.limit || 9);
        const q = typeof req.query.q === "string" ? req.query.q.trim() : "";

        if (
            !Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1 ||
            !Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 24 ||
            q.length > 100
        ) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                status: "error",
                message: "Invalid pagination or search parameters."
            });
        }

        const filter = {
            status: "ACTIVE",
            isDeleted: { $ne: true }
        };

        if (q) {
            const escapedQuery = q.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
            filter.$or = ["name", "address", "city", "state", "pincode"].map(
                (field) => ({
                    [field]: {
                        $regex: escapedQuery,
                        $options: "i"
                    }
                })
            );
        }

        const publicFields =
            "name photoUrl galleryImages address city state pincode contact email roomSetup pricing createdAt";

        const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
            Hostel.find(filter)
                .select(publicFields)
                .sort({ createdAt: -1, _id: 1 })
                .skip((page - 1) * limit)
                .limit(limit)
                .lean(),
            Hostel.countDocuments(filter)
        ]);

        return res.status(200).json({
            status: "success",
            data: {
                items,
                pagination: {
                    page,
                    limit,
                    total,
                    pages: Math.ceil(total / limit)
                }
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const archiveById = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne({ _id: req.params.id, isDeleted: { $ne: true } });
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        const hostelId = hostel._id;
        const activeStructure = { hostelId, isDeleted: { $ne: true } };
        const activeStudent = { ...activeStructure, status: "ACTIVE" };
        const activeAllocation = { hostelId, status: "ACTIVE" };
        const dependent = await Promise.all([
            Block.exists(activeStructure),
            Floor.exists(activeStructure),
            Room.exists(activeStructure),
            Bed.exists(activeStructure),
            Student.exists(activeStudent),
            Allocation.exists(activeAllocation)
        ]);
        if (dependent.some(Boolean)) {
            return res.status(409).json({ status: "error", message: "Archive active students, allocations, blocks, floors, rooms, and beds before archiving this hostel. Historical records are retained." });
        }
        hostel.status = "INACTIVE";
        hostel.isDeleted = true;
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: safeHostel(hostel) });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};
