const mongoose = require("mongoose");
const Hostel = require("../models/Hostel");
const Block = require("../models/Block");
const Floor = require("../models/Floor");
const Room = require("../models/Room");
const Bed = require("../models/Bed");
const Student = require("../models/Student");
const Allocation = require("../models/Allocation");
const hostelScope = require("../utils/hostelScope");

const SAFE_FIELDS = "name hostelCode photoUrl galleryImages address city state pincode contact email roomSetup status createdAt updatedAt";
const httpError = (statusCode, message) => Object.assign(new Error(message), { statusCode });
const safeHostel = (hostel) => {
    const record = typeof hostel.toObject === "function" ? hostel.toObject() : { ...hostel };
    delete record.accessCode;
    delete record.ownerId;
    return record;
};
const isImageUrl = (value) => {
    if (!value) return true;
    try {
        const url = new URL(value);
        return url.protocol === "http:" || url.protocol === "https:";
    } catch {
        return false;
    }
};

const getCurrent = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const fields = req.auth?.role === "OWNER" ? `${SAFE_FIELDS} accessCode` : SAFE_FIELDS;
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne(hostelScope(req, { isDeleted: { $ne: true } })).select(fields).lean();
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        return res.status(200).json({
            status: "success",
            data: { ...hostel, ...(req.auth?.role === "OWNER" ? {} : { accessCode: undefined }) }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const updateCurrent = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const allowed = ["name", "photoUrl", "galleryImages", "address", "city", "state", "pincode", "contact", "email", "roomSetup"];
        const body = req.body;
        if (!body || typeof body !== "object" || Array.isArray(body)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "A JSON object is required." });
        const unknown = Object.keys(body).filter((key) => !allowed.includes(key));
        if (unknown.length) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `Unsupported field: ${unknown[0]}.` });
        if (!Object.keys(body).length) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Provide at least one hostel field to update." });
        const update = {};
        for (const field of allowed) {
            if (!Object.hasOwn(body, field)) continue;
            const value = body[field];
            if (["galleryImages"].includes(field)) {
                if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length > 8 || value.some((url) => typeof url !== "string" || url.length > 2048 || !isImageUrl(url))) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "galleryImages must contain up to 8 valid HTTP(S) image URLs." });
                }
                update[field] = value.map((url) => url.trim());
            } else if (field === "photoUrl") {
                if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > 2048 || !(isImageUrl(value) || /^\/api\/uploads\/photos\/[a-f\d-]{36}\.(?:jpg|png|webp)$/i.test(value))) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "photoUrl must be an uploaded photo reference or valid HTTP(S) URL." });
                update[field] = value.trim();
            } else if (field === "roomSetup") {
                if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "roomSetup must be an object." });
                const { totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom, roomTypes } = value;
                if (![totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom].every((number) => Number.isInteger(number) && number >= 0 && number <= 100000) || !Array.isArray(roomTypes) || roomTypes.length > 8 || roomTypes.some((type) => typeof type !== "string" || !type.trim() || type.length > 64)) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "roomSetup contains invalid capacity or room type values." });
                }
                update[field] = { totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom, roomTypes: roomTypes.map((type) => type.trim()) };
            } else {
                const maxLength = field === "address" ? 512 : 128;
                if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim().length > maxLength) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `${field} must be a string of at most ${maxLength} characters.` });
                if (["name"].includes(field) && !value.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `${field} cannot be empty.` });
                if (field === "email" && value.trim() && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(value.trim())) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "email must be a valid email address." });
                update[field] = value.trim();
            }
        }
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne(hostelScope(req, { isDeleted: { $ne: true } }));
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        hostel.set(update);
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: hostel.toObject({ transform: (doc, ret) => { delete ret.accessCode; delete ret.ownerId; return ret; } }) });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error.statusCode) return res.status(error.statusCode).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
        if (error.name === "ValidationError") return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
        return next(error);
    }
};

const updateById = async (req, res, next) => {
    if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
    req.tenant = { hostelId: req.params.id };
    return updateCurrent(req, res, next);
};

const listAll = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const page = Number(req.query.page || 1);
        const limit = Number(req.query.limit || 25);
        const q = typeof req.query.q === "string" ? req.query.q.trim() : "";
        if (!Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1 || !Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100 || q.length > 100) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid pagination or search parameters." });
        const filter = { isDeleted: req.query.archived === "true" ? true : { $ne: true } };
        if (q) filter.$or = ["name", "hostelCode", "city", "state"].map((field) => ({ [field]: { $regex: q.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&"), $options: "i" } }));
        const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
            Hostel.find(filter).select(SAFE_FIELDS).sort({ createdAt: -1 }).skip((page - 1) * limit).limit(limit).lean(),
            Hostel.countDocuments(filter)
        ]);
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: { items, pagination: { page, limit, total, pages: Math.ceil(total / limit) } } });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const archiveById = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne({ _id: req.params.id, isDeleted: { $ne: true } });
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        const hostelId = hostel._id;
        const activeStructure = { hostelId, isDeleted: { $ne: true } };
        const activeStudent = { ...activeStructure, status: "ACTIVE" };
        const activeAllocation = { hostelId, status: "ACTIVE" };
        const dependent = await Promise.all([
            Block.exists(activeStructure),
            Floor.exists(activeStructure),
            Room.exists(activeStructure),
            Bed.exists(activeStructure),
            Student.exists(activeStudent),
            Allocation.exists(activeAllocation)
        ]);
        if (dependent.some(Boolean)) {
            return res.status(409).json({ status: "error", message: "Archive active students, allocations, blocks, floors, rooms, and beds before archiving this hostel. Historical records are retained." });
        }
        hostel.status = "INACTIVE";
        hostel.isDeleted = true;
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: safeHostel(hostel) });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const restoreById = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne({ _id: req.params.id, isDeleted: true });
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Archived hostel not found." });
        hostel.status = "ACTIVE";
        hostel.isDeleted = false;
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: safeHostel(hostel) });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const getById = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne({ _id: req.params.id, isDeleted: { $ne: true } }).select(SAFE_FIELDS).lean();
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: hostel });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const setStatus = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
        if (!req.body || !["ACTIVE", "INACTIVE"].includes(req.body.status)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "status must be ACTIVE or INACTIVE." });
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOneAndUpdate(
            { _id: req.params.id, isDeleted: { $ne: true } },
            { $set: { status: req.body.status } },
            { new: true, runValidators: true }
        ).select(SAFE_FIELDS).lean();
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: hostel });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};
module.exports = { archiveById, getCurrent, getById, listAll, restoreById, setStatus, updateById, updateCurrent };

const Hostel = require("../models/Hostel");
const Block = require("../models/Block");
const Floor = require("../models/Floor");

const Bed = require("../models/Bed");
const Student = require("../models/Student");
const Allocation = require("../models/Allocation");

const SAFE_FIELDS = "name hostelCode photoUrl galleryImages address city state pincode contact email roomSetup pricing status createdAt updatedAt";
const httpError = (statusCode, message) => Object.assign(new Error(message), { statusCode });

    delete record.ownerId;

const updateCurrent = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const allowed = ["name", "photoUrl", "galleryImages", "address", "city", "state", "pincode", "contact", "email", "roomSetup", "pricing"];
        const body = req.body;
        if (!body || typeof body !== "object" || Array.isArray(body)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "A JSON object is required." });
        const unknown = Object.keys(body).filter((key) => !allowed.includes(key));
        if (unknown.length) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `Unsupported field: ${unknown[0]}.` });
        if (!Object.keys(body).length) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Provide at least one hostel field to update." });
        const update = {};
        for (const field of allowed) {
            if (!Object.hasOwn(body, field)) continue;
            const value = body[field];
            if (["galleryImages"].includes(field)) {
                if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length > 8 || value.some((url) => typeof url !== "string" || url.length > 2048 || !isImageUrl(url))) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "galleryImages must contain up to 8 valid HTTP(S) image URLs." });
                }
                update[field] = value.map((url) => url.trim());
            } else if (field === "photoUrl") {
                if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > 2048 || !(isImageUrl(value) || /^\/api\/uploads\/photos\/[a-f\d-]{36}\.(?:jpg|png|webp)$/i.test(value))) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "photoUrl must be an uploaded photo reference or valid HTTP(S) URL." });
                update[field] = value.trim();
            } else if (field === "pricing") {
                if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "pricing must be an object." });
                }

                const amountFields = [
                    "singleSharing", "doubleSharing", "tripleSharing", "fourSharing",
                    "securityDeposit", "messCharges"
                ];
                const supportedFields = [...amountFields, "messIncluded"];
                const unknownField = Object.keys(value).find((key) => !supportedFields.includes(key));

                if (unknownField) {
                    return res.status(400).json({
                        status: "error",
                        message: `Unsupported pricing field: ${unknownField}.`
                    });
                }

                const cleaned = {};

                for (const key of amountFields) {
                    const amount = value[key];
                    if (amount === undefined || amount === null || amount === "") {
                        cleaned[key] = null;
                        continue;
                    }

                    const minimum = ["securityDeposit", "messCharges"].includes(key) ? 0 : 1;
                    if (typeof amount !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount < minimum || amount > 10000000) {
                        return res.status(400).json({
                            status: "error",
                            message: `${key} must be a valid INR amount.`
                        });
                    }
                    cleaned[key] = amount;
                }

                if (
                    value.messIncluded !== undefined &&
                    value.messIncluded !== null &&
                    typeof value.messIncluded !== "boolean"
                ) {
                    return res.status(400).json({
                        status: "error",
                        message: "messIncluded must be true, false, or null."
                    });
                }

                cleaned.messIncluded = value.messIncluded ?? null;
                update[field] = cleaned;
            } else if (field === "roomSetup") {
                if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "roomSetup must be an object." });
                const { totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom, roomTypes } = value;
                if (![totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom].every((number) => Number.isInteger(number) && number >= 0 && number <= 100000) || !Array.isArray(roomTypes) || roomTypes.length > 8 || roomTypes.some((type) => typeof type !== "string" || !type.trim() || type.length > 64)) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "roomSetup contains invalid capacity or room type values." });
                }
                update[field] = { totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom, roomTypes: roomTypes.map((type) => type.trim()) };
            } else {
                const maxLength = field === "address" ? 512 : 128;
                if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim().length > maxLength) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `${field} must be a string of at most ${maxLength} characters.` });
                if (["name"].includes(field) && !value.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `${field} cannot be empty.` });
                if (field === "email" && value.trim() && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(value.trim())) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "email must be a valid email address." });
                update[field] = value.trim();
            }
        }
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne(hostelScope(req, { isDeleted: { $ne: true } }));
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        hostel.set(update);
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: hostel.toObject({ transform: (doc, ret) => { delete ret.accessCode; delete ret.ownerId; return ret; } }) });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error.statusCode) return res.status(error.statusCode).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
        if (error.name === "ValidationError") return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
        return next(error);
    }
};

const updateById = async (req, res, next) => {
    if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
    req.tenant = { hostelId: req.params.id };

const listPublic = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const page = Number(req.query.page || 1);
        const limit = Number(req.query.limit || 9);
        const q = typeof req.query.q === "string" ? req.query.q.trim() : "";

        if (
            !Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1 ||
            !Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 24 ||
            q.length > 100
        ) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                status: "error",
                message: "Invalid pagination or search parameters."
            });
        }

        const filter = {
            status: "ACTIVE",
            isDeleted: { $ne: true }
        };

        if (q) {
            const escapedQuery = q.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
            filter.$or = ["name", "address", "city", "state", "pincode"].map(
                (field) => ({
                    [field]: {
                        $regex: escapedQuery,
                        $options: "i"
                    }
                })
            );
        }

        const publicFields =
            "name photoUrl galleryImages address city state pincode contact email roomSetup pricing createdAt";

        const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
            Hostel.find(filter)
                .select(publicFields)
                .sort({ createdAt: -1, _id: 1 })
                .skip((page - 1) * limit)
                .limit(limit)
                .lean(),
            Hostel.countDocuments(filter)
        ]);

        return res.status(200).json({
            status: "success",
            data: {
                items,
                pagination: {
                    page,
                    limit,
                    total,
                    pages: Math.ceil(total / limit)
                }
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const mongoose = require("mongoose");
const Hostel = require("../models/Hostel");
const Block = require("../models/Block");
const Floor = require("../models/Floor");
const Room = require("../models/Room");
const Bed = require("../models/Bed");
const Student = require("../models/Student");
const Allocation = require("../models/Allocation");
const hostelScope = require("../utils/hostelScope");

const SAFE_FIELDS = "name hostelCode photoUrl galleryImages address city state pincode contact email roomSetup status createdAt updatedAt";
const httpError = (statusCode, message) => Object.assign(new Error(message), { statusCode });
const safeHostel = (hostel) => {
    const record = typeof hostel.toObject === "function" ? hostel.toObject() : { ...hostel };
    delete record.accessCode;
    delete record.ownerId;
    return record;

        return res.status(200).json({
            status: "success",
            data: { ...hostel, ...(req.auth?.role === "OWNER" ? {} : { accessCode: undefined }) }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const updateCurrent = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const allowed = ["name", "photoUrl", "galleryImages", "address", "city", "state", "pincode", "contact", "email", "roomSetup"];
        const body = req.body;
        if (!body || typeof body !== "object" || Array.isArray(body)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "A JSON object is required." });
        const unknown = Object.keys(body).filter((key) => !allowed.includes(key));
        if (unknown.length) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `Unsupported field: ${unknown[0]}.` });
        if (!Object.keys(body).length) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Provide at least one hostel field to update." });
        const update = {};
        for (const field of allowed) {
            if (!Object.hasOwn(body, field)) continue;
            const value = body[field];
            if (["galleryImages"].includes(field)) {
                if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length > 8 || value.some((url) => typeof url !== "string" || url.length > 2048 || !isImageUrl(url))) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "galleryImages must contain up to 8 valid HTTP(S) image URLs." });
                }
                update[field] = value.map((url) => url.trim());
            } else if (field === "photoUrl") {
                if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > 2048 || !(isImageUrl(value) || /^\/api\/uploads\/photos\/[a-f\d-]{36}\.(?:jpg|png|webp)$/i.test(value))) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "photoUrl must be an uploaded photo reference or valid HTTP(S) URL." });
                update[field] = value.trim();
            } else if (field === "roomSetup") {
                if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "roomSetup must be an object." });
                const { totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom, roomTypes } = value;
                if (![totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom].every((number) => Number.isInteger(number) && number >= 0 && number <= 100000) || !Array.isArray(roomTypes) || roomTypes.length > 8 || roomTypes.some((type) => typeof type !== "string" || !type.trim() || type.length > 64)) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "roomSetup contains invalid capacity or room type values." });
                }
                update[field] = { totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom, roomTypes: roomTypes.map((type) => type.trim()) };
            } else {
                const maxLength = field === "address" ? 512 : 128;
                if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim().length > maxLength) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `${field} must be a string of at most ${maxLength} characters.` });
                if (["name"].includes(field) && !value.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `${field} cannot be empty.` });
                if (field === "email" && value.trim() && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(value.trim())) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "email must be a valid email address." });
                update[field] = value.trim();
            }
        }
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne(hostelScope(req, { isDeleted: { $ne: true } }));
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        hostel.set(update);
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: hostel.toObject({ transform: (doc, ret) => { delete ret.accessCode; delete ret.ownerId; return ret; } }) });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error.statusCode) return res.status(error.statusCode).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
        if (error.name === "ValidationError") return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
        return next(error);
    }
};

const updateById = async (req, res, next) => {
    if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
    req.tenant = { hostelId: req.params.id };
    return updateCurrent(req, res, next);
};

const listPublic = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const page = Number(req.query.page || 1);
        const limit = Number(req.query.limit || 9);
        const q = typeof req.query.q === "string" ? req.query.q.trim() : "";

        if (
            !Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1 ||
            !Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 24 ||
            q.length > 100
        ) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                status: "error",
                message: "Invalid pagination or search parameters."
            });
        }

        const filter = {
            status: "ACTIVE",
            isDeleted: { $ne: true }
        };

        if (q) {
            const escapedQuery = q.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
            filter.$or = ["name", "address", "city", "state", "pincode"].map(
                (field) => ({
                    [field]: {
                        $regex: escapedQuery,
                        $options: "i"
                    }
                })
            );
        }

        const publicFields =
            "name photoUrl galleryImages address city state pincode contact email roomSetup createdAt";

        const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
            Hostel.find(filter)
                .select(publicFields)
                .sort({ createdAt: -1, _id: 1 })
                .skip((page - 1) * limit)
                .limit(limit)
                .lean(),
            Hostel.countDocuments(filter)
        ]);

        return res.status(200).json({
            status: "success",
            data: {
                items,
                pagination: {
                    page,
                    limit,
                    total,
                    pages: Math.ceil(total / limit)
                }
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};
const listAll = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const page = Number(req.query.page || 1);
        const limit = Number(req.query.limit || 25);
        const q = typeof req.query.q === "string" ? req.query.q.trim() : "";
        if (!Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1 || !Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100 || q.length > 100) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid pagination or search parameters." });
        const filter = { isDeleted: req.query.archived === "true" ? true : { $ne: true } };
        if (q) filter.$or = ["name", "hostelCode", "city", "state"].map((field) => ({ [field]: { $regex: q.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&"), $options: "i" } }));
        const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
            Hostel.find(filter).select(SAFE_FIELDS).sort({ createdAt: -1 }).skip((page - 1) * limit).limit(limit).lean(),
            Hostel.countDocuments(filter)
        ]);
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: { items, pagination: { page, limit, total, pages: Math.ceil(total / limit) } } });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const archiveById = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne({ _id: req.params.id, isDeleted: { $ne: true } });
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        const hostelId = hostel._id;
        const activeStructure = { hostelId, isDeleted: { $ne: true } };
        const activeStudent = { ...activeStructure, status: "ACTIVE" };
        const activeAllocation = { hostelId, status: "ACTIVE" };
        const dependent = await Promise.all([
            Block.exists(activeStructure),
            Floor.exists(activeStructure),
            Room.exists(activeStructure),
            Bed.exists(activeStructure),
            Student.exists(activeStudent),
            Allocation.exists(activeAllocation)
        ]);
        if (dependent.some(Boolean)) {
            return res.status(409).json({ status: "error", message: "Archive active students, allocations, blocks, floors, rooms, and beds before archiving this hostel. Historical records are retained." });
        }
        hostel.status = "INACTIVE";
        hostel.isDeleted = true;
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: safeHostel(hostel) });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const restoreById = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne({ _id: req.params.id, isDeleted: true });
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Archived hostel not found." });
        hostel.status = "ACTIVE";
        hostel.isDeleted = false;
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: safeHostel(hostel) });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const getById = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne({ _id: req.params.id, isDeleted: { $ne: true } }).select(SAFE_FIELDS).lean();
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: hostel });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const setStatus = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
        if (!req.body || !["ACTIVE", "INACTIVE"].includes(req.body.status)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "status must be ACTIVE or INACTIVE." });
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOneAndUpdate(
            { _id: req.params.id, isDeleted: { $ne: true } },
            { $set: { status: req.body.status } },
            { new: true, runValidators: true }
        ).select(SAFE_FIELDS).lean();
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: hostel });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};
module.exports = { archiveById, getCurrent, getById, listAll, listPublic, restoreById, setStatus, updateById, updateCurrent };

    const record = typeof hostel.toObject === "function" ? hostel.toObject() : { ...hostel };
    delete record.accessCode;
    delete record.ownerId;
    return record;

    if (!value) return true;
    try {
        const url = new URL(value);
        return url.protocol === "http:" || url.protocol === "https:";
    } catch {
        return false;
    }

        return res.status(200).json({
            status: "success",
            data: { ...hostel, ...(req.auth?.role === "OWNER" ? {} : { accessCode: undefined }) }
        });

        return next(error);
    }

    try {
        const allowed = ["name", "photoUrl", "galleryImages", "address", "city", "state", "pincode", "contact", "email", "roomSetup"];
        const body = req.body;
        if (!body || typeof body !== "object" || Array.isArray(body)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "A JSON object is required." });
        const unknown = Object.keys(body).filter((key) => !allowed.includes(key));
        if (unknown.length) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `Unsupported field: ${unknown[0]}.` });
        if (!Object.keys(body).length) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Provide at least one hostel field to update." });
        const update = {};
        for (const field of allowed) {
            if (!Object.hasOwn(body, field)) continue;
            const value = body[field];
            if (["galleryImages"].includes(field)) {
                if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length > 8 || value.some((url) => typeof url !== "string" || url.length > 2048 || !isImageUrl(url))) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "galleryImages must contain up to 8 valid HTTP(S) image URLs." });
                }
                update[field] = value.map((url) => url.trim());
            } else if (field === "photoUrl") {
                if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > 2048 || !(isImageUrl(value) || /^\/api\/uploads\/photos\/[a-f\d-]{36}\.(?:jpg|png|webp)$/i.test(value))) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "photoUrl must be an uploaded photo reference or valid HTTP(S) URL." });
                update[field] = value.trim();
            } else if (field === "roomSetup") {
                if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "roomSetup must be an object." });
                const { totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom, roomTypes } = value;
                if (![totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom].every((number) => Number.isInteger(number) && number >= 0 && number <= 100000) || !Array.isArray(roomTypes) || roomTypes.length > 8 || roomTypes.some((type) => typeof type !== "string" || !type.trim() || type.length > 64)) {
                    return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "roomSetup contains invalid capacity or room type values." });
                }
                update[field] = { totalRooms, totalBeds, bedsPerRoom, roomTypes: roomTypes.map((type) => type.trim()) };
            } else {
                const maxLength = field === "address" ? 512 : 128;
                if (typeof value !== "string" || value.trim().length > maxLength) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `${field} must be a string of at most ${maxLength} characters.` });
                if (["name"].includes(field) && !value.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: `${field} cannot be empty.` });
                if (field === "email" && value.trim() && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(value.trim())) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "email must be a valid email address." });
                update[field] = value.trim();
            }
        }
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne(hostelScope(req, { isDeleted: { $ne: true } }));
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        hostel.set(update);
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: hostel.toObject({ transform: (doc, ret) => { delete ret.accessCode; delete ret.ownerId; return ret; } }) });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error.statusCode) return res.status(error.statusCode).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
        if (error.name === "ValidationError") return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: error.message });
        return next(error);
    }
};

const updateById = async (req, res, next) => {
    if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
    req.tenant = { hostelId: req.params.id };
    return updateCurrent(req, res, next);
};

const listPublic = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        const page = Number(req.query.page || 1);
        const limit = Number(req.query.limit || 9);
        const q = typeof req.query.q === "string" ? req.query.q.trim() : "";

        if (
            !Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1 ||
            !Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 24 ||
            q.length > 100
        ) {
            return res.status(400).json({
                status: "error",
                message: "Invalid pagination or search parameters."
            });
        }

        // Only active, non-archived hostels are visible in the public directory.
        const filter = {
            status: "ACTIVE",
            isDeleted: { $ne: true }
        };

        if (q) {
            const escapedQuery = q.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
            filter.$or = ["name", "address", "city", "state", "pincode"].map(
                (field) => ({
                    [field]: {
                        $regex: escapedQuery,
                        $options: "i"
                    }
                })
            );
        }

        // Explicit projection excludes ownerId and accessCode.
        const publicFields =
            "name photoUrl galleryImages address city state pincode contact email roomSetup createdAt";

        const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
            Hostel.find(filter)
                .select(publicFields)
                .sort({ createdAt: -1, _id: 1 })
                .skip((page - 1) * limit)
                .limit(limit)
                .lean(),
            Hostel.countDocuments(filter)
        ]);

        return res.status(200).json({
            status: "success",
            data: {
                items,
                pagination: {
                    page,
                    limit,
                    total,
                    pages: Math.ceil(total / limit)
                }
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

        const filter = { isDeleted: req.query.archived === "true" ? true : { $ne: true } };

        const [items, total] = await Promise.all([

            Hostel.countDocuments(filter)
        ]);
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: { items, pagination: { page, limit, total, pages: Math.ceil(total / limit) } } });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);

const archiveById = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne({ _id: req.params.id, isDeleted: { $ne: true } });
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Hostel not found." });
        const hostelId = hostel._id;
        const activeStructure = { hostelId, isDeleted: { $ne: true } };
        const activeStudent = { ...activeStructure, status: "ACTIVE" };
        const activeAllocation = { hostelId, status: "ACTIVE" };
        const dependent = await Promise.all([
            Block.exists(activeStructure),
            Floor.exists(activeStructure),
            Room.exists(activeStructure),
            Bed.exists(activeStructure),
            Student.exists(activeStudent),
            Allocation.exists(activeAllocation)
        ]);
        if (dependent.some(Boolean)) {
            return res.status(409).json({ status: "error", message: "Archive active students, allocations, blocks, floors, rooms, and beds before archiving this hostel. Historical records are retained." });
        }
        hostel.status = "INACTIVE";
        hostel.isDeleted = true;
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: safeHostel(hostel) });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

const restoreById = async (req, res, next) => {
    try {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(req.params.id)) return res.status(400).json({ status: "error", message: "Invalid hostel identifier." });
        const hostel = await Hostel.findOne({ _id: req.params.id, isDeleted: true });
        if (!hostel) return res.status(404).json({ status: "error", message: "Archived hostel not found." });
        hostel.status = "ACTIVE";
        hostel.isDeleted = false;
        await hostel.save();
        return res.status(200).json({ status: "success", data: safeHostel(hostel) });
    } catch (error) {
        return next(error);
    }
};

FILE: .\server\src\middleware\tenantMiddleware.js.bak_multihostel_20261016_121335

const Hostel = require("../models/Hostel");
const ROLES = require("../constants/roles");

const tenantForbidden = (res) => res.status(403).json({
    status: "error",
    message: "You do not have access to this hostel"
});

const tenantMiddleware = async (req, res, next) => {
    if (!req.auth?.role) {
        return res.status(401).json({
            status: "error",
            message: "Authentication required"
        });
    }

    const requestedHostelId = req.get("x-hostel-id");

    if (req.auth.role === ROLES.SYSTEM_ADMIN) {
        const selectedHostelId = requestedHostelId || req.auth.hostelId;
        if (!selectedHostelId) {
            req.tenant = null;
            return next();
        }

        try {
            const hostel = await Hostel.findOne({
                _id: selectedHostelId,
                status: "ACTIVE",
                isDeleted: { $ne: true }
            }).select("_id").lean();

            if (!hostel) {
                return tenantForbidden(res);
            }

            req.tenant = { hostelId: String(hostel._id) };
            return next();
        } catch (error) {
            if (error.name === "CastError") {
                return tenantForbidden(res);
            }
            return next(error);
        }
    }

    const memberships = req.auth.memberships || [];
    const selectedHostelId = requestedHostelId || req.auth.hostelId;

    if (!selectedHostelId || !memberships.includes(selectedHostelId)) {
        return tenantForbidden(res);
    }

    req.tenant = { hostelId: selectedHostelId };
    return next();
};

const requireTenant = (req, res, next) => {
    if (!req.tenant?.hostelId) {
        return res.status(403).json({
            status: "error",
            message: "A hostel context is required"
        });
    }

    return next();
};

module.exports = {
    requireTenant,
    tenantMiddleware
};

const mongoose = require("mongoose");

const allocationSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
    hostelId: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Hostel", required: true, index: true },
    studentId: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Student", required: true },
    bedId: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Bed", required: true },
    roomId: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Room", required: true, index: true },
    allocatedBy: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "User", required: true },
    allocationDate: { type: Date, required: true, default: Date.now },
    releasedAt: { type: Date, default: null },
    releasedBy: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "User", default: null },
    status: { type: String, enum: ["ACTIVE", "RELEASED"], default: "ACTIVE", index: true },
    transferFrom: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: "Allocation", default: null }
}, { timestamps: true });

allocationSchema.index({ hostelId: 1, studentId: 1, status: 1 });
allocationSchema.index({ hostelId: 1, bedId: 1, status: 1 });
allocationSchema.index({ studentId: 1 }, { unique: true, partialFilterExpression: { status: "ACTIVE" } });
allocationSchema.index({ bedId: 1 }, { unique: true, partialFilterExpression: { status: "ACTIVE" } });

module.exports = mongoose.model("Allocation", allocationSchema);

            payeeName: { type: String, trim: true, default: "" }

        status: { type: String, enum: ["ACTIVE", "INACTIVE"], default: "ACTIVE", index: true },

const express = require("express");
const controller = require("../controllers/allocationController");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/authMiddleware");
const requireRoles = require("../middleware/roleMiddleware");
const ROLES = require("../constants/roles");
const { requireTenant, tenantMiddleware } = require("../middleware/tenantMiddleware");

const router = express.Router();
router.use(requireAuth, requireRoles(ROLES.SYSTEM_ADMIN, ROLES.OWNER, ROLES.WARDEN), tenantMiddleware, requireTenant);
router.get("/options", controller.options);
router.get("/available-beds", controller.availableBeds);
router.get("/", controller.list);
router.post("/", controller.assign);
router.post("/:id/release", controller.release);
// DELETE safely releases the active bed assignment and preserves allocation history.
router.delete("/:id", controller.release);
router.post("/:id/transfer", controller.transfer);
module.exports = router;

const requireAuth = require("../middleware/authMiddleware");
const requireRoles = require("../middleware/roleMiddleware");
const { tenantMiddleware } = require("../middleware/tenantMiddleware");

const router = express.Router();

router.get(
    "/summary",
    requireAuth,
    requireRoles("SYSTEM_ADMIN", "OWNER", "WARDEN"),
    tenantMiddleware,
    dashboardController.getSummary
);

module.exports = router;

const express = require("express");
const requireAuth = require("../middleware/authMiddleware");
const requireRoles = require("../middleware/roleMiddleware");
const { requireTenant, tenantMiddleware } = require("../middleware/tenantMiddleware");
const controller = require("../controllers/facilityController");
const ROLES = require("../constants/roles");

const router = express.Router();
router.use(requireAuth, requireRoles(ROLES.SYSTEM_ADMIN, ROLES.OWNER, ROLES.WARDEN), tenantMiddleware, requireTenant);

for (const resource of ["blocks", "floors", "rooms", "beds"]) {
    const collection = express.Router();
    collection.get("/", (req, res, next) => { req.params.resource = resource; return controller.list(req, res, next); });
    collection.post("/", (req, res, next) => { req.params.resource = resource; return controller.create(req, res, next); });
    collection.get("/:id", (req, res, next) => { req.params.resource = resource; return controller.get(req, res, next); });
    collection.patch("/:id", (req, res, next) => { req.params.resource = resource; return controller.update(req, res, next); });
    collection.delete("/:id", (req, res, next) => { req.params.resource = resource; return controller.archive(req, res, next); });
    router.use(`/${resource}`, collection);
}

module.exports = router;

======================================================================
FILE: .\server\src\services\allocationService.js
======================================================================
const mongoose = require("mongoose");
const Student = require("../models/Student");
const Block = require("../models/Block");
const Floor = require("../models/Floor");
const Room = require("../models/Room");
const Bed = require("../models/Bed");
const Allocation = require("../models/Allocation");
const hostelScope = require("../utils/hostelScope");
const httpError = (statusCode, message) => Object.assign(new Error(message), { statusCode });
const validId = mongoose.isValidObjectId;

const allocationDate = (value) => {
    if (value === undefined || value === "") return new Date();
    const date = new Date(value);
    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) || date.getTime() > Date.now()) throw httpError(400, "allocationDate must be a valid date that is not in the future.");
    return date;
};
const identifier = (value, field) => {
    if (typeof value !== "string" || !validId(value)) throw httpError(400, `${field} must be a valid identifier.`);
    return value;
};
const activeRecord = { isDeleted: { $ne: true } };

const syncRoomStatus = async (session, hostelId, roomId) => {
    const [room, occupied] = await Promise.all([
        Room.findOne({ _id: roomId, hostelId, ...activeRecord }).session(session),
        Allocation.countDocuments({ hostelId, roomId, status: "ACTIVE" }).session(session)
    ]);
    if (room && !["MAINTENANCE", "RESERVED"].includes(room.status)) {
        room.status = occupied > 0 ? "OCCUPIED" : "AVAILABLE";
        await room.save({ session });
    }
};

const options = async (req) => {
    const hostelId = req.tenant.hostelId;
    const [blocks, floors, rooms, beds] = await Promise.all([
        Block.find({ hostelId, ...activeRecord, status: "ACTIVE" }).select("name").sort({ name: 1 }).lean(),
        Floor.find({ hostelId, ...activeRecord, status: "ACTIVE" }).select("name number blockId").sort({ number: 1 }).lean(),
        Room.find({ hostelId, ...activeRecord }).select("roomNumber roomType capacity status blockId floorId").sort({ roomNumber: 1 }).lean(),
        Bed.find({ hostelId, ...activeRecord, status: "AVAILABLE" }).select("bedNumber roomId status").sort({ bedNumber: 1 }).lean()
    ]);
    return { blocks, floors, rooms, availableBeds: beds };
};

const list = async (req) => {
    const page = Number(req.query.page || 1);
    const limit = Number(req.query.limit || 25);
    if (!Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1 || !Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100) throw httpError(400, "Invalid pagination parameters.");
    const filter = hostelScope(req, { ...activeRecord, status: "ACTIVE" });
    const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
        Allocation.find(filter).populate("studentId", "name studentId mobile").populate({ path: "bedId", select: "bedNumber roomId", populate: { path: "roomId", select: "roomNumber roomType" } }).sort({ allocationDate: -1 }).skip((page - 1) * limit).limit(limit).lean(),
        Allocation.countDocuments(filter)
    ]);
    return { items, pagination: { page, limit, total, pages: Math.ceil(total / limit) } };
};

const listAvailableBeds = async (req) => {
    const roomId = identifier(req.query.roomId, "roomId");
    const room = await Room.findOne(hostelScope(req, { _id: roomId, ...activeRecord })).select("_id capacity status").lean();
    if (!room) throw httpError(404, "Room not found in this hostel.");
    if (["MAINTENANCE", "RESERVED"].includes(room.status)) throw httpError(409, "This room is not available for allocation.");
    return Bed.find(hostelScope(req, { roomId, status: "AVAILABLE", ...activeRecord })).select("bedNumber roomId status").sort({ bedNumber: 1 }).lean();
};

const verifyHierarchy = async (hostelId, bed, requestedRoomId, session) => {
    const room = await Room.findOne({ _id: requestedRoomId, hostelId, ...activeRecord }).session(session).lean();
    if (!room || String(bed.roomId) !== String(room._id)) throw httpError(404, "Bed does not belong to the selected room in this hostel.");
    if (["MAINTENANCE", "RESERVED"].includes(room.status)) throw httpError(409, "Cannot allocate a bed in a room that is under maintenance or reserved.");
    const [block, floor] = await Promise.all([
        Block.findOne({ _id: room.blockId, hostelId, ...activeRecord, status: "ACTIVE" }).session(session).select("_id").lean(),
        Floor.findOne({ _id: room.floorId, hostelId, ...activeRecord, status: "ACTIVE" }).session(session).select("_id blockId").lean()
    ]);
    if (!block || !floor || String(floor.blockId) !== String(room.blockId)) throw httpError(409, "Room hierarchy is inactive or inconsistent.");
    const bedCount = await Bed.countDocuments({ hostelId, roomId: room._id, ...activeRecord }).session(session);
    if (bedCount > room.capacity) throw httpError(409, "The room exceeds its configured bed capacity. Correct the room inventory before allocation.");
    return room;
};

const withTransaction = async (operation) => {
    const session = await mongoose.startSession();
    try { return await session.withTransaction(() => operation(session)); }
    catch (error) {
        if (error.statusCode) throw error;
        if (error.code === 11000 || error.code === 112 || error.codeName === "WriteConflict") throw httpError(409, "The student or bed was changed by another request. Refresh and try again.");
        throw error;
    } finally { await session.endSession(); }
};

const assign = async (req) => {
    const studentId = identifier(req.body?.studentId, "studentId");
    const bedId = identifier(req.body?.bedId, "bedId");
    const roomId = identifier(req.body?.roomId, "roomId");
    if (req.body?.hostelId && String(req.body.hostelId) !== req.tenant.hostelId) throw httpError(403, "The requested hostel does not match your verified hostel context.");
    const allocatedOn = allocationDate(req.body?.allocationDate);
    return withTransaction(async (session) => {
        const hostelId = req.tenant.hostelId;
        const student = await Student.findOne({ _id: studentId, hostelId, status: "ACTIVE", bedId: null, ...activeRecord }).session(session);
        if (!student) throw httpError(409, "Student is unavailable in this hostel or already has an active bed.");
        const bed = await Bed.findOne({ _id: bedId, hostelId, roomId, status: "AVAILABLE", ...activeRecord }).session(session);
        if (!bed) throw httpError(409, "Bed is unavailable or does not belong to the selected room and hostel.");
        const room = await verifyHierarchy(hostelId, bed, roomId, session);
        const occupancy = await Allocation.countDocuments({ hostelId, roomId, status: "ACTIVE" }).session(session);
        if (occupancy >= room.capacity) throw httpError(409, "Room capacity has been reached.");
        const changedBed = await Bed.updateOne({ _id: bed._id, hostelId, status: "AVAILABLE", ...activeRecord }, { $set: { status: "OCCUPIED" } }, { session });
        if (changedBed.modifiedCount !== 1) throw httpError(409, "Bed is no longer available. Refresh and try again.");
        student.blockId = room.blockId;
        student.floorId = room.floorId;
        student.roomId = room._id;
        student.bedId = bed._id;
        await student.save({ session });
        const allocation = new Allocation({ hostelId, studentId: student._id, bedId: bed._id, roomId: room._id, allocatedBy: req.auth.id, allocationDate: allocatedOn, status: "ACTIVE" });
        await allocation.save({ session });
        await syncRoomStatus(session, hostelId, room._id);
        return { allocation: allocation.toObject(), student: { id: String(student._id), name: student.name, studentId: student.studentId }, room: { id: String(room._id), roomNumber: room.roomNumber }, bed: { id: String(bed._id), bedNumber: bed.bedNumber } };
    });
};

const findActiveAllocation = async (id, req, session) => {
    if (!validId(id)) throw httpError(400, "Invalid allocation identifier.");
    const allocation = await Allocation.findOne(hostelScope(req, { _id: id, status: "ACTIVE" })).session(session);
    if (!allocation) throw httpError(404, "Active allocation not found.");
    return allocation;
};

const release = async (req) => withTransaction(async (session) => {
    const allocation = await findActiveAllocation(req.params.id, req, session);
    const now = new Date();
    const student = await Student.findOne({ _id: allocation.studentId, hostelId: req.tenant.hostelId, bedId: allocation.bedId, ...activeRecord }).session(session);
    const bed = await Bed.findOne({ _id: allocation.bedId, hostelId: req.tenant.hostelId, status: "OCCUPIED", ...activeRecord }).session(session);
    if (!student || !bed) throw httpError(409, "Student and bed allocation state is inconsistent; no records were changed.");
    student.blockId = null; student.floorId = null; student.roomId = null; student.bedId = null;
    bed.status = "AVAILABLE";
    allocation.status = "RELEASED"; allocation.releasedAt = now; allocation.releasedBy = req.auth.id;
    await Promise.all([student.save({ session }), bed.save({ session }), allocation.save({ session })]);
    await syncRoomStatus(session, req.tenant.hostelId, allocation.roomId);
    return { released: true, studentId: String(student._id), bedId: String(bed._id), releasedAt: now };
});

const transfer = async (req) => {
    const bedId = identifier(req.body?.bedId, "bedId");
    const roomId = identifier(req.body?.roomId, "roomId");
    return withTransaction(async (session) => {
        const previous = await findActiveAllocation(req.params.id, req, session);
        if (String(previous.bedId) === bedId) throw httpError(400, "Select a different destination bed.");
        const student = await Student.findOne({ _id: previous.studentId, hostelId: req.tenant.hostelId, bedId: previous.bedId, status: "ACTIVE", ...activeRecord }).session(session);
        const oldBed = await Bed.findOne({ _id: previous.bedId, hostelId: req.tenant.hostelId, status: "OCCUPIED", ...activeRecord }).session(session);
        const newBed = await Bed.findOne({ _id: bedId, roomId, hostelId: req.tenant.hostelId, status: "AVAILABLE", ...activeRecord }).session(session);
        if (!student || !oldBed) throw httpError(409, "Current student and bed allocation state is inconsistent.");
        if (!newBed) throw httpError(409, "Destination bed is unavailable or outside this hostel.");
        const room = await verifyHierarchy(req.tenant.hostelId, newBed, roomId, session);
        const occupancy = await Allocation.countDocuments({ hostelId: req.tenant.hostelId, roomId, status: "ACTIVE" }).session(session);
        if (String(previous.roomId) !== roomId && occupancy >= room.capacity) throw httpError(409, "Destination room capacity has been reached.");
        const now = new Date();
        oldBed.status = "AVAILABLE"; newBed.status = "OCCUPIED";
        previous.status = "RELEASED"; previous.releasedAt = now; previous.releasedBy = req.auth.id;
        student.blockId = room.blockId; student.floorId = room.floorId; student.roomId = room._id; student.bedId = newBed._id;
        const nextAllocation = new Allocation({ hostelId: req.tenant.hostelId, studentId: student._id, bedId: newBed._id, roomId: room._id, allocatedBy: req.auth.id, allocationDate: now, status: "ACTIVE", transferFrom: previous._id });
        await Promise.all([oldBed.save({ session }), newBed.save({ session }), previous.save({ session }), student.save({ session }), nextAllocation.save({ session })]);
        await syncRoomStatus(session, req.tenant.hostelId, previous.roomId);
        await syncRoomStatus(session, req.tenant.hostelId, room._id);
        return { allocation: nextAllocation.toObject(), student: { id: String(student._id), name: student.name, studentId: student.studentId }, room: { id: String(room._id), roomNumber: room.roomNumber }, bed: { id: String(newBed._id), bedNumber: newBed.bedNumber } };
    });
};

module.exports = { assign, httpError, list, listAvailableBeds, options, release, transfer, allocationDate, verifyHierarchy };
